---
title: "When AI agents pay suppliers: the B2B payouts desk"
summary: "How AI agents can pay suppliers without paying the wrong one. Rules catch, AI flags, and a rule or a person releases. The guards every agentic payout needs, the bank and stablecoin rails it runs on, and the rules that set who carries the loss. Research, not advice."
pubDate: 2026-10-24
geography: United States weighted, with European Union and Brazil rules where they bind a payout
horizon: 0 to 2 years

<nav class="pdd-rail" aria-label="Screens"><a href="#pdd-answer" aria-label="The answer for your seat"></a><a href="#pdd-ai" aria-label="Where AI earns a place"></a><a href="#pdd-build" aria-label="How agent payments get built"></a><a href="#pdd-path" aria-label="The money path"></a><a href="#pdd-chooser" aria-label="Which rail fits"></a><a href="#pdd-rules" aria-label="Rules and open questions"></a></nav>
<section class="pdd-scene pdd-gate" id="pdd-gate" aria-label="Choose your seat">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>0</b>Start here</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q">Where do you sit?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a"><b class="pdd-s1-lead">Rules catch, AI flags, a rule or a person releases.</b> Six screens answer for your seat.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: what AI saves, where money is lost, what to check first.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: where AI agents fit the build, the states and the rail logic.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: who earns from AI agents, what is priced, which rules move the market.</span></p>
<p class="pdd-viewkey"><span class="pdd-view">Highlighted lines</span> are our read.</p>
<p class="pdd-viewkey pdd-tagkey">Tags on this page. <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live">Live</span> build now. <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot">Pilot</span> adapter behind a flag. <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-tag="spec">Spec</span> build to the token. <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled">Unsettled</span> contract clause. <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> use your own numbers. <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor">Vendor</span> ask for evidence.</p>
<div class="pdd-readers" role="group" aria-label="Choose your seat">
<button type="button" class="pdd-reader" data-reader-btn="buyer" aria-pressed="false"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-coin"/></svg><b>Finance buyer</b><span>You run finance or accounts payable and pay suppliers.</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-reader" data-reader-btn="builder" aria-pressed="false"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-plug"/></svg><b>Product builder</b><span>You build a payout or payables product and choose its rails.</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-reader" data-reader-btn="strategy" aria-pressed="false"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg><b>Strategy reader</b><span>You track where control and income sit in payments.</span></button>
</div>
<a class="pdd-skip" href="#pdd-answer">Skip to the answer</a>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-gloss"><summary>Glossary</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul pdd-gloss-l"><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-key"/></svg><b>Mandate</b> a signed record of what the payer approved: who, how much, until when.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-rails"/></svg><b>Bank rail</b> ACH, RTP, FedNow or Fedwire. Money moves between bank accounts.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-vault"/></svg><b>For-benefit account</b> a bank account a platform holds for its customers, kept apart from its own money.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-coin"/></svg><b>Stablecoin rail</b> a dollar token moves between wallets, then an off-ramp turns it back into bank money.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-gateway"/></svg><b>Off-ramp</b> the service that turns a stablecoin into money in a bank account.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-shield"/></svg><b>Payee guard</b> the rules that check a new or changed supplier before any money goes out.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-matrix"/></svg><b>Release matrix</b> the table that says when a rule may release a payment and when a person must.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-people"/></svg><b>Checker</b> the second person who releases a payment the rule will not.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-ledger"/></svg><b>Evidence ledger</b> the write-once record of who approved what, and the proof the payment landed.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-plug"/></svg><b>Rail adapter</b> the one piece of code allowed to send a signed release to the bank.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-lock"/></svg><b>Idempotency key</b> a one-time key on each payment, so a retry cannot pay twice.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-power"/></svg><b>Kill switch</b> one control that stops an agent, or all agent payments, with our target of under a minute.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-card"/></svg><b>Network token</b> a stand-in for the card number that a seller can hold safely.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-agent"/></svg><b>Agentic token</b> a network token tied to one agent, with a spend cap, an allowed seller and an expiry.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-check"/></svg><b>Name check</b> the bank confirms the payee name matches the account before paying. EU and UK schemes.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-globe"/></svg><b>Travel Rule</b> at $3,000 or more, sender and payee details travel with a wire or a transmitter transfer.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-wall"/></svg><b>SDN list</b> the US Treasury list of sanctioned people and firms every payment is screened against.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg><b>Nacha</b> the body that writes the ACH rules, including the 2026 fraud monitoring rule.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-payer"/></svg><b>Originator</b> the business that starts an ACH payment. Nacha gives it its own fraud monitoring duty.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-policy"/></svg><b>GENIUS Act</b> the 2025 US law for payment stablecoin issuers, in force from 18 Jan 2027.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-policy-doc"/></svg><b>UCC 4A</b> the US law on business payment orders. The payer carries the loss if the bank followed its procedure.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-email"/></svg><b>Email compromise</b> a fraudster poses as a supplier by email and asks for new bank details.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-payer"/></svg><b>Take rate</b> the fee a provider keeps per dollar moved. 52 basis points is 0.52%.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-sheet"/></svg><b>Basis points</b> hundredths of a percent. 100 basis points is 1%.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-rtp"/></svg><b>Finality</b> the point after which a payment cannot be pulled back. RTP is final at once, ACH is not.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-security"/></svg><b>Visa TAP</b> Visa Trusted Agent Protocol. The agent signs its web traffic so a seller knows who it is.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-gateway"/></svg><b>Mastercard Agent Pay</b> Mastercard agentic tokens that tie a card to one agent.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-mcp"/></svg><b>Google AP2 and OpenAI ACP</b> two draft standards for the mandate an agent carries into checkout.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg><b>x402</b> pay per web call over HTTP, now a Linux Foundation project.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-hsm"/></svg><b>Web Bot Auth</b> the signature standard, RFC 9421, that Visa, Mastercard and Amex use to recognize an agent.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-globe"/></svg><b>FSB</b> Financial Stability Board, the G20 body that tracks risk across the world banking system.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-coin"/></svg><b>FX</b> Foreign exchange, swapping one currency for another at a rate plus a spread.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg><b>MAS</b> Monetary Authority of Singapore, the city state central bank and regulator.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-shield"/></svg><b>FinCEN</b> The US Treasury unit that writes anti money laundering rules and receives suspicious activity reports.</li><li><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="32" height="32" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-ledger"/></svg><b>SOX</b> Sarbanes-Oxley, the US law that makes a company prove its financial controls work.</li></ul></details>
<noscript><p class="pdd-a">The diagrams on this page need JavaScript. The first screen shows the finance buyer view.</p></noscript>
</section>
<section class="pdd-scene pdd-s1-scene" id="pdd-answer" aria-label="The answer for your seat">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>1</b>Answer</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q">What is the answer for your seat?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a"><b class="pdd-s1-lead">Rules catch, AI flags, a rule or a person releases.</b> Three questions, each with a verdict, one number and the screen that proves it.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: what your desk saves, who releases, and who carries the loss.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: where agents assist, and what to build first.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: who earns, and which rules to watch.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<div class="pdd-grid pdd-s1-row">
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="buyer" data-m="cost" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-sheet"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Your AP desk</span><span class="pdd-stamp pdd-view">Your numbers decide</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">What would your AP desk save?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#18" data-note="Default case of our AP desk model: 3,000 suppliers, 72,000 payments a year, 60% released by rule, $9.40 cost per payment from the WEX benchmark, a $400K build, $15K a year AWS run cost and $50K fraud loss. Illustration, not a quote.">$240K</span></span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">a year in the default case, payback in month 21</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-note="Our arithmetic. Change every input on the Delivery tab.">Illustrative</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">3,000 suppliers, 72,000 payments a year, 60% released by rule. Move the sliders on the Delivery tab to your own numbers.</p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-build">Delivery tab<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="buyer" data-m="ok" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-approve"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Release</span><span class="pdd-stamp pdd-view">A person still releases</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">Who releases the money?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num" data-s1-count><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#10 W#11 W#12 N#8" data-note="Meow, Payouts.com, Tipalti and the Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot let the agent go last, each inside limits a person set. Every other shipped product stops at an approval queue.">4</span></span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">vendors let the agent go last, inside limits a person set</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#10" data-note="Meow blog, 10 Jun 2026. Keys default to read and request-to-spend. An agent spends on its own only if the account holder turns it on, inside per-agent limits. Vendor page.">Meow</span> spend keys the account holder switches on, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#11" data-note="Payouts.com Digital Employee, 14 Jul 2026: hard spend limits, multi-party sign-off on large wires, two weeks of shadow mode first. Trade press.">Payouts.com</span> after shadow mode, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#12" data-note="Tipalti AP Agent, 22 Oct 2026. Finance teams set which activities the agent handles alone and which need human approval.">Tipalti</span> configurable autonomy, and the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot, 20 Oct 2026. The agent only executes payments the buyer has already approved.">Visa, BILL and Billtrust</span> pilot after buyer approval. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#4 W#7 W#8 W#9" data-note="Stripe Treasury for agents, Coupa, Ramp and Oracle each stop at an approval queue where a person releases.">Every other shipped product</span> stops at the approval queue.</p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-rules">Who releases<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="buyer" data-m="risk" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-shield"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Risk</span><span class="pdd-stamp pdd-view">You carry the loss</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">Who carries the loss?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num" data-s1-count><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#1" data-note="FBI IC3 2025: reported business email compromise losses, all channels, 24,768 complaints.">$3.05B</span></span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">lost to business email compromise in 2025, FBI</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">About <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#1" data-note="Our arithmetic: $3.05B divided by 24,768 complaints.">$123,000</span> per complaint. Under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6" data-note="UCC 4A-202(b): an order counts as the customer&#39;s whether or not authorized, if the agreed security procedure was commercially reasonable and the bank followed it in good faith.">UCC 4A</span>, the US law on business payment orders, the loss stays with you if your bank followed the agreed security procedure. <span class="pdd-view">Check bank detail changes first.</span></p><div class="pdd-s1-gos"><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-path">Money path<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-s5-ask" data-open="#pdd-s5-ask">Ask your bank<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></div></article>
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="builder" data-m="flow" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-agent"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>AI</span><span class="pdd-stamp pdd-view">Assist, never release</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">Where does AI earn a place today?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num" data-s1-count><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#36" data-note="Finch benchmark, Findings of ACL 2026. Every frontier system tested passed fewer than half.">38.4%</span></span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">of workflows passed, best agent</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">Agents propose coding, triage and repair. A rule or a person keeps <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#30" data-note="As reported at Sibos 2026, through a newsletter citing a vendor story.">payment release</span>.</span></p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-ai">Where AI fits<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="builder" data-m="coin" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-coin"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Stablecoin</span><span class="pdd-stamp pdd-view">Optional, added last</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">Does the stablecoin rail earn a place?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num">2 of 10</span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">payout states differ</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-note="Our model">Illustrative</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">Both rails share eight of ten payout states, so the stablecoin rail is one adapter behind the same instruction.</p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-path">Money path<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="builder" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-key"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Build</span><span class="pdd-stamp pdd-view">Identity, then rails</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">What to build first?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num pdd-s1-words">Identity, ACH and RTP</span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">year one, through the bank API you already use</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">A signed agent identity first, then ACH and RTP through your bank API. The card lane is pilot-ready, and a release matrix decides who releases.<span class="pdd-s1-sub">Resends: an unknown outcome is resent only after the rail confirms the payment is absent, with the same key. Stripe may prune keys after <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#29" data-note="Stripe API v1 replays the first response for a repeated key. Keys can be pruned after this.">24 hours</span>.</span></p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-build">Verdict tab<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="strategy" data-m="flow" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-agent"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>AI</span><span class="pdd-stamp">No price per outcome</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">Where does AI earn a place today?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num pdd-s1-words">Bundled, not priced</span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">Three 2026 market sizes run <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#2" data-note="Future Market Insights, 2026 estimate. Sells the report.">$3.8B</span> to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#3" data-note="The Business Research Company, 2026 estimate. Its own table shows a conflicting growth rate.">$6.57B</span>, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#1 J#2 J#3" data-note="Our arithmetic: $6.57 billion divided by $3.8 billion, the highest and lowest 2026 estimates for AP automation.">1.7 times</span> apart</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">BILL, Ramp and Tipalti bundle AI into <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#21 J#22 J#23" data-note="Price lists as read in early Oct 2026.">plans</span>.</span> No price list we found charges per outcome.</p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-ai">Where AI fits<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="strategy" data-m="coin" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-coin"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Stablecoin</span><span class="pdd-stamp pdd-view">Small</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">Does the stablecoin rail earn a place?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num pdd-s1-words">Small for payouts</span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">About <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#9" data-note="Our arithmetic: about $400 billion times 60%. Both inputs are Stripe&#39;s own.">$240 billion</span> of B2B stablecoin payments in 2025, our arithmetic, beside <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#2" data-note="Nacha: ACH payments in 2025 were worth $93 trillion.">$93 trillion</span> of ACH</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">Stripe says stablecoin payments reached about <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#9" data-note="Stripe&#39;s figure for 2025.">$400 billion</span> in 2025, about <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#9" data-note="Stripe&#39;s own estimate.">60%</span> of it B2B. The FSB puts the coin market at <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#1" data-note="FSB note to the G20, 12 Oct 2026: about $300 billion as of mid-2026, 99% dollar-pegged.">$300 billion</span>.</span> No firm stablecoin payout price exists.</p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-chooser">Rail chooser<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>
<article class="pdd-card pdd-c4 pdd-s1-tile" data-for="strategy" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s1-head"><svg class="pdd-s1-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="88" height="88" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-policy"/></svg><div class="pdd-s1-verdict"><span class="pdd-label"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Rules</span><span class="pdd-stamp pdd-view">Watch liability</span></div></div><h3 class="pdd-s1-q">What to watch first?</h3><p class="pdd-s1-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-s1-num pdd-s1-words">Liability is unsettled</span><span class="pdd-s1-cap">A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#27" data-note="Governor Waller, speech of 29 Sep 2026.">Fed governor</span> names liability as a barrier to agent payments</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-why">Take rates fell at <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#14 J#13" data-note="Wise fiscal 2026 and Payoneer second quarter 2026.">Wise and Payoneer</span>.</span> Wise kept <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#14" data-note="Wise, fiscal year to 31 Mar 2026. Wise states this take rate, down 6 basis points on the year, so 58 a year earlier.">52 basis points</span> per dollar moved in fiscal 2026, down from 58.</p><a class="pdd-pill pdd-s1-go" href="#pdd-rules">Rules<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg></a></article>
</div>
<ul class="pdd-bd-ul pdd-s1-reads" data-for="strategy" aria-label="Three reads for the strategy seat"><li><span class="pdd-view">Our read: networks own the first two checks on an agent payment. Whoever owns the third owns the payer.</span> <a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2</a></li><li><span class="pdd-view">Our read: the networks are writing the rulebook before regulators do.</span> <a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-path">Screen 4</a></li><li><span class="pdd-view">Our read: the release split matters more than rail coverage, and it will decide who wins payouts.</span> <a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-rules">Screen 6</a> <a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-s5-deals" data-open="#pdd-s5-deals">Who buys what</a></li></ul>
</div>
</section>
<section class="pdd-scene pdd-s4-scene" id="pdd-ai" aria-labelledby="pdd-s4-q">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>2</b>AI agents</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q" id="pdd-s4-q">Where does AI earn a place?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">Four of nine use cases run in production on vendor numbers, and two stay unsettled.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: which AI saves money today, the three checks, and the guard.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: what each agent does, and who releases.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: where agent volume and pricing stand.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<div class="pdd-ag" id="pdd-agents" aria-label="Agent payment checks, standards and where agents pay today">
<div class="pdd-ag-foot"><details class="pdd-more pdd-vg-checks" data-open-for="all"><summary>Three checks</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-security"/></svg></span><b>Card is real</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="V#1 V#6" data-note="Network tokens are in production at the card networks. VGS describes the three checks.">Live</span> A network token proves the card exists and is open.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-agent"/></svg></span><b>Owner or agent</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="V#5 V#6 V#2" data-note="Agent registration and signed intent are in pilot at Visa, Mastercard and Amex.">Pilot</span> Agent sign-up and signed intent show who asked.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-approve"/></svg></span><b>Agent got it right</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-src="V#1" data-note="No network checks this. Your release step does.">Illustrative</span> Your release step and payee rules answer this one.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">The first two checks belong to the networks. The third one is yours.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Ask each vendor which of the three checks they cover today.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Log the signed intent and token ID next to each release.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: Networks own the first two checks. Whoever owns the third owns the payer.</span></p></details><details class="pdd-more pdd-ag-who"><summary>Who pays</summary><ul class="pdd-s5-al"><li>Under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#1" data-note="Goodwin, Jun 2026. A law firm reading of UCC Article 4A.">UCC 4A</span>, <span class="pdd-view">a business payment generally binds the payer if the bank followed the agreed security procedure.</span></li><li>Melio says its <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#2" data-note="A Melio claim, launched 17 Jun 2026. A vendor figure.">agent payment network</span> is live for small businesses.</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#3" data-note="Deloitte research, Mar 2026, as reported by MarketScale.">38%</span> of B2B buyers already use agentic AI in purchasing.</li><li>The same payee guard applies. An agent should never edit bank details or release money. <a href="#pdd-guard">Payee guard</a></li></ul></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-vg-std"><summary>Checkout standards</summary><p class="pdd-label pdd-ag-top">Where agents pay today</p><div class="pdd-ag-row">
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-ag-tile" data-m="flow" data-rise><div class="pdd-ag-head"><svg class="pdd-ag-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-agent"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-ag-name">B2B supplier agents</h3></div><p class="pdd-ag-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-ag-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#2" data-note="Melio: over $100M annualized agent payment volume and 30,000 payments, both in the 17 Jun 2026 launch release. A vendor figure.">&gt;$100M</span></span><span class="pdd-ag-cap">a year at one network</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Vendor figure">Vendor</span></p><p class="pdd-ag-now"><b>Today</b> Melio says live since <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#2" data-note="Melio launched its agent payment network on 17 Jun 2026. Melio&#39;s own claim.">Jun 2026</span> <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Vendor claim">Vendor</span>. A Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot began <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8 N#9" data-note="Visa calls it the first live pilot through supplier portals. Visa&#39;s own claim. No liability framework is set.">20 Oct 2026</span>.</p><dl class="pdd-ag-out"><dt>Problem</dt><dd>An agent can pay the wrong supplier.</dd><dt>Root cause</dt><dd>Only <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#3" data-note="Deloitte research, Mar 2026, as reported by MarketScale. Suppliers whose front and back office are completely integrated.">13%</span> of suppliers are fully integrated.</dd><dt>Our read, 6 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">More payables tools ship it. A person approves payee changes.</span></dd><dt>Our read, 12 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Banks add agent rules to payment terms.</span></dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-ag-tile" data-m="coin" data-rise><div class="pdd-ag-head"><svg class="pdd-ag-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-card"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-ag-name">Consumer agent checkout</h3></div><p class="pdd-ag-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-ag-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4" data-note="Global merchant chargeback costs in 2026, a Mastercard forecast cited by Chargeflow. No agent checkout market is sized yet.">$36.9B</span></span><span class="pdd-ag-cap">chargebacks agent rules must cover</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Vendor forecast">Vendor</span></p><p class="pdd-ag-now"><b>Today</b> Live at <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#8 M#7" data-note="Shopify opened Shop MCP on 13 Oct 2026. Google began a US rollout on 15 Oct 2026.">Shopify and Google</span>.</span> ChatGPT moved checkout to retailer sites in <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#17" data-note="ForkLog, Mar 2026. Shoppers now finish on retailer sites.">Mar 2026</span>.</span> Buyers still confirm.</p><dl class="pdd-ag-out"><dt>Problem</dt><dd>An agent buys the wrong thing, or too much.</dd><dt>Root cause</dt><dd>Card <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4" data-note="Chargeflow, Jul 2026, a vendor blog. Reason codes describe the cardholder&#39;s claim, not the agent&#39;s role.">dispute rules</span> were written for human buyers.</dd><dt>Our read, 6 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">More volume.</span></dd><dt>Our read, 12 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Card disputes weigh agent signals.</span></dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-ag-tile" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-ag-head"><svg class="pdd-ag-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-plug"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-ag-name">x402 pay per call</h3></div><p class="pdd-ag-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-ag-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="AP#1" data-note="Single operator tracker, monthly total for Oct 2026. Trackers disagree on totals.">$274K</span></span><span class="pdd-ag-cap">in Oct 2026</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Tracker estimate, trackers disagree">Vendor</span></p><p class="pdd-ag-now"><b>Today</b> A Linux Foundation project with <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#6" data-note="x402 Foundation, 1 Oct 2026.">50+ members</span>. Volume is <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#5" data-note="Crypto Briefing reports activity fell from its 2025 highs.">below its peak</span>.</p><dl class="pdd-ag-out"><dt>Problem</dt><dd>Agents pay per API call, with no account.</dd><dt>Root cause</dt><dd>Tickets average <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="AP#1" data-note="Our arithmetic from one tracker's monthly totals, Aug to Oct 2026: about 2.5 to 5.4 cents per transaction. Raw counts include tests and self dealing.">a few cents</span>,</span> <span class="pdd-view">too small for card fees.</span></dd><dt>Our read, 6 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">A few million a month at most.</span></dd><dt>Our read, 12 months</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Grows only if Google AP2 adds new rails.</span></dd></dl></article>
</div><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-gateway"/></svg></span><b>Mastercard Agent Pay</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-src="N#18 V#6" data-note="Mastercard newsroom. Consumer payments in Europe. No B2B release found.">Vendor</span> Agentic tokens tie a card to one agent. Consumer only so far.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="id"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-security"/></svg></span><b>Visa TAP</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="V#5 N#22" data-note="Visa Trusted Agent Protocol, 14 Oct 2025, in pilot with partners.">Pilot</span> Visa Trusted Agent Protocol signs agent traffic so sellers know it.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-policy-doc"/></svg></span><b>Google AP2</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-tag="spec" data-src="N#15 V#7" data-note="AP2 v0.2 specification. No pilot source found.">Spec</span> Signed mandates for what the user approved.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-mcp"/></svg></span><b>OpenAI ACP</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-tag="spec" data-src="N#16 V#8" data-note="Agentic Commerce Protocol, beta spec with Shared Payment Tokens. No pilot source found.">Spec</span> Shared payment tokens, scoped to one seller.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-sdk"/></svg></span><b>Amex ACE</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="V#2" data-note="American Express ACE developer kit and Agent Purchase Protection, 14 Apr 2026.">Pilot</span> A kit for select developers since Apr 2026.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg></span><b>x402</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="V#3 M#6" data-note="x402 Foundation under the Linux Foundation, Apr 2026. Volumes are self-reported.">Live</span> Pay per call over HTTP, now a Linux Foundation project.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-vault"/></svg></span><b>Wallets</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="V#1 V#8" data-note="Which wallet holds the agent token is not settled. VGS and Stripe describe the race.">Unsettled</span> Link, Shop Pay and PayPal race to hold the card.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">Our read: no single standard wins soon. Build to the token, not the protocol.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Ask vendors which tokens they accept now, not which protocols they plan.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Build one token adapter so a new protocol is a plug, not a rebuild.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: Whoever holds the token wallet controls which agent can pay.</span></p></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-nw-task"><summary>Pay per task</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-mcp"/></svg></span><b>Nano payments</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-src="DC#7 N#27" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026. x402 tickets average about 32 cents.">Illustrative</span> Tiny payments per call or per task, too small for cards to carry.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-approve"/></svg></span><b>Conditional pay</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-src="DC#7 DC#11" data-note="x402 has no escrow. Escrow sits in separate layers, per a protocol comparison blog.">Illustrative</span> Pay the supplier only when delivery is confirmed. Needs a separate escrow layer today.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">Small and conditional payments suit agents better than invoices.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Try conditional release on one supplier group with clear delivery proof.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Store the condition and the proof with each payment record.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: Conditional pay will reach payouts before any agent lending does.</span></p></details>
</div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-grid">

<div class="pdd-panel pdd-s4-detail" data-g="pv" data-m="ok" aria-live="polite">
<div class="pdd-s4-dhead"><h3 class="pdd-s4-dname">Invoice coding</h3><span class="pdd-s4-badge">Vendor, in production with vendor numbers</span><span class="pdd-s4-lever">Moves: Share of cost saved</span></div>
<dl class="pdd-s4-dl">
<dt class="pdd-label">Problem</dt><dd data-row="p"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#18" data-note="WEX restates a 2025 Ardent Partners benchmark. Best in class is $2.78.">$9.40</span> to process an average invoice, WEX reports.</dd>
<dt class="pdd-label">What AI does</dt><dd data-row="a">Reads the invoice and proposes coding.</dd>
<dt class="pdd-label">Who keeps control</dt><dd data-row="k"><span class="pdd-view">A person approves uncertain coding.</span></dd>
<dt class="pdd-label">Evidence</dt><dd data-row="e">BILL: <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#10 H#13" data-note="BILL Invoice Coding Agent, Feb 2026. A vendor figure, and BILL says results may vary.">up to 89% fewer multi line coding steps</span>.</span> The W-9 Agent claim is separate: over 80% of a 12-step process.</dd>
</dl>
</div>
</div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s4-right"><summary>Market size</summary>
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-s4-range" data-m="flow">
<p class="pdd-label">AP automation market, 2026</p>
<ul class="pdd-s4-bars" aria-label="Three published sizes for the same market run from 3.8 to 6.57 billion dollars, 1.7 times apart">
<li style="--v:54.3%"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#2" data-note="Future Market Insights, 2026 estimate. Sells the report.">$3.8B</span><span>Future Market Insights</span></li>
<li style="--v:91.4%"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#1" data-note="Research and Markets, 2026 estimate. Sells the report.">$6.4B</span><span>Research and Markets</span></li>
<li style="--v:93.9%"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#3" data-note="Its own table shows a conflicting growth rate.">$6.57B</span><span>The Business Research Company</span></li>
</ul>
<p class="pdd-s4-fine"><span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#2 J#3" data-note="Our arithmetic: 6.57 / 3.8 = 1.73.">1.7 times apart</span>.</span> These size a market, unlike the sum below.</p>
</div>

Paper checks, wires and cards are outside. The slider range is an illustration. Q2 2026, all ACH: 9.3 billion payments worth $25.9 trillion.">B2B ACH, 2025</span></p><output for="pdd-s4-pay">8.1B</output><input type="range" id="pdd-s4-pay" min="1" max="12" step="0.1" value="8.1"></div>

The range is an illustration.">Exception rate proxy</span></p><output for="pdd-s4-auto">14%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-s4-auto" min="5" max="100" step="1" value="14"></div>

Markers: about 1% was AI-linked, and the FBI froze 58% of attempted theft it acted on.">of $3.05B</span></p><output for="pdd-s4-fraud">25%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-s4-fraud" min="1" max="60" step="1" value="25"></div>

<ul class="pdd-s4-leg"><li data-m="cost"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Processing <b data-out="proc">$3.73B</b></li><li data-m="risk"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Fraud <b data-out="fraud">$0.76B</b></li></ul>
</div>
</div>
</details>
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-pg" id="pdd-guard" data-m="risk" aria-label="Payee guard">
<div class="pdd-pg-head"><h3>Payee guard</h3><p class="pdd-pg-tl pdd-view">Rules catch, AI flags, a rule or a person releases.</p></div>
<ol class="pdd-pg-strip"><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="rule"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-email"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">1</span><b>Capture any channel</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Rule</span><p>Email, portal, phone, ERP edits and new vendors all enter. Nothing goes live yet.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="ai"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-doc"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">2</span><b>Read request</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">AI</span><p>AI flags warning signs. It can add a hold and nothing more.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="rule"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">3</span><b>Check account</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Rule</span><p>A rule checks who owns the account, the name, how old it is, and whether the bank or country changed.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="ai"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-risk"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">4</span><b>Score risk</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Rule and AI</span><p>Scores a first payment, an unusual amount or a recent change of contact.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="person"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-phone"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">5</span><b>Verify out of band</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Person</span><p>Call a number older than any change, or a public registry for new vendors.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="person"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-lock"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">6</span><b>Hold and release</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Person</span><p>A second person releases. The longest hold time is published to suppliers.</p></li><li class="pdd-pg-step" data-a="person"><span class="pdd-pg-ico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-check"/></svg></span><span class="pdd-pg-n">7</span><b>Confirm receipt</b><span class="pdd-pg-a">Person</span><p>Confirm the money arrived through the old contact. If fraud is caught, freeze, recall and report it.</p></li></ol>
<p class="pdd-pg-ev">No independent result yet for AI on this step. Baltimore sent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1" data-note="Baltimore 2025: vendor Workday account taken over. $1.52M sent, $721K recovered, $803K lost, insurance claim filed.">$1.5 million</span> to a changed vendor account after staff approved the change without checking documents. <span class="pdd-view">Our read: a callback would have stopped it. That is our assumption.</span></p>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-pg-more"><summary>Three personas</summary><div class="pdd-pg-mw"><table class="pdd-pg-t"><thead><tr><th scope="col">Gap</th><th scope="col">Raised by</th><th scope="col">Fix</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Portal and ERP changes skip the email read</td><td>CFO, Investigator, Supplier</td><td>Every channel enters the guard. Nothing goes live before the callback.</td></tr><tr><td>The number on file was changed first, or the call is deepfaked</td><td>All three</td><td>Cooling period on contact changes. Confirm a code sent to a second channel.</td></tr><tr><td>One insider edits, calls back and releases</td><td>Investigator</td><td>Split the duties. Overrides need two named approvers.</td></tr><tr><td>A real but hacked mailbox, or a mule account in the same name, passes</td><td>Investigator</td><td>A clean read never lowers risk. Weight account age and bank change.</td></tr><tr><td>False positives hold honest suppliers</td><td>CFO, Supplier</td><td>Run in shadow mode first. Publish a maximum hold and show payees the status.</td></tr><tr><td>No owner, no evidence an insurer would accept</td><td>CFO, Investigator</td><td>Name one owner. Keep a record that shows how you meet the Nacha 2026 rule (ACH only).</td></tr><tr><td>Bulk vendor imports skip the guard</td><td>Investigator</td><td>Bulk imports go through the same checks. Phone agents cannot edit bank fields.</td></tr><tr><td>Slow-burn changes pass the first-payment hold</td><td>Investigator, CFO</td><td>Keep new details on probation. Confirm receipt through the old contact.</td></tr><tr><td>Honest edge cases: time zones, bank mergers, no name-check scheme</td><td>Supplier</td><td>Bookable callback slots. A missing name-check scheme means a callback, never a neutral score.</td></tr></tbody></table><p class="pdd-pg-weak">Biggest weakness: the callback trusts a record the attacker may already have changed.</p><p class="pdd-pg-fine">Personas are reasoning roles, not interviews.</p></div></details>
</div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s4-cuts" data-m="risk"><summary>Against it</summary>
<ul class="pdd-s4-tiles">
<li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#30" data-note="Gartner survey of 204 finance leaders, Mar 2026, as republished.">63%</span><span>slower AI rollout than expected, Gartner reports</span></li>
<li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#31" data-note="Sample size is not stated on the page.">35%</span><span>measure AI return well, Protiviti reports</span></li>
<li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#36" data-note="Finch benchmark. Every frontier system passed fewer than half.">38.4%</span><span>of finance and accounting workflows passed, best agent</span></li>
<li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#29" data-note="Revised interagency guidance, 17 Apr 2026.">Out of scope</span><span>generative AI, in US model risk guidance</span></li>
</ul>
</details>
</div>

<section class="pdd-scene pdd-bd-scene" id="pdd-build" aria-labelledby="pdd-bd-q" data-tab="verdict">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>3</b>The build</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q" id="pdd-bd-q">How agent payments get built</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">Twelve steps carry one agent payment. Your payment core checks the mandate, the signed record of what the payer approved. A rule checks the payee. A release matrix, the table that says when a rule may release and when a person must, picks who releases each payment. Consumer checkout and every agent payment protocol are compared in <a href="/deep-dives/agentic-commerce-protocols">Who Pays When the Agent Buys</a>.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: what each party gains, and what to ask your bank.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: the AWS layout, the seven checks and the integrations.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: who gains, who loses and the verdict.</span></p>
