architecture map, scroll to pan"></div><div class="pdd-ar3-prog" aria-hidden="true"></div><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar3-legd" data-open-for="all"><summary>Legend</summary><ul class="pdd-ar3-legend" aria-label="Legend"><li><svg width="44" height="14" aria-hidden="true"><line x1="2" y1="7" x2="42" y2="7" class="pdd-ar3-lk" data-k="main"/></svg><span>Payment path</span></li><li><svg width="44" height="14" aria-hidden="true"><line x1="2" y1="7" x2="42" y2="7" class="pdd-ar3-lk" data-k="rule"/></svg><span>Rule releases</span></li><li><svg width="44" height="14" aria-hidden="true"><line x1="2" y1="7" x2="42" y2="7" class="pdd-ar3-lk" data-k="person"/></svg><span>Person releases</span></li><li><svg width="44" height="14" aria-hidden="true"><line x1="2" y1="7" x2="42" y2="7" class="pdd-ar3-lk" data-k="id"/></svg><span>Identity</span></li><li><svg width="44" height="14" aria-hidden="true"><line x1="2" y1="7" x2="42" y2="7" class="pdd-ar3-lk" data-k="ctrl"/></svg><span>Control</span></li><li><svg width="44" height="14" aria-hidden="true"><line x1="2" y1="7" x2="42" y2="7" class="pdd-ar3-lk" data-k="event"/></svg><span>Returns and events</span></li><li><svg width="44" height="14" aria-hidden="true"><line x1="2" y1="7" x2="42" y2="7" class="pdd-ar3-lk" data-k="coin"/></svg><span>Stablecoin, gated</span></li><li><span class="pdd-ar3-seal is-key"><svg viewBox="-13 -13 26 26" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle r="10"/><path d="M-5 0l3.5 3.5 6.5-7"/></svg></span><span>Signed release</span></li><li><span class="pdd-ar3-lock is-key"><svg viewBox="-13 -13 26 26" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M-5 -2v-3a5 5 0 0 1 10 0v3M-6.5 -2h13v9h-13z"/></svg></span><span>Release role only</span></li><li><svg width="22" height="22" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M11 1 21 11 11 21 1 11z" fill="#e0a030"/></svg><span>SOX key control</span></li><li><svg width="22" height="22" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="9" class="pdd-ar3-lport"/></svg><span>Portable</span></li><li><svg width="22" height="22" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="2" y="2" width="18" height="18" rx="3" fill="#ff9900"/></svg><span>AWS-bound</span></li></ul></details></div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-ar3-stepsd"><summary>Step by step</summary><ol class="pdd-ar3-steps" data-st="today" aria-label="Six steps today">
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c1" data-s="1" data-m="flow"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">1</b><span>Invoice by email</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Supplier invoices, and any bank detail change, arrive by email.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">1</b> <b>Spoofable.</b> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#7" data-note="AFP 2026 survey: 74% of firms were hit by email compromise in 2025.">74%</span> of firms were hit by email compromise in 2025.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>The agent drafts only from an approved invoice, step 2.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c2" data-s="2" data-m="flow"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">2</b><span>AP clerk in ERP</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>A clerk keys the invoice and the payee into the ERP.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Cost</dt><dd>About <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#26 I#19" data-note="WEX restates a $9.40 average invoice cost and $2.78 best in class.">$9.40</span> to process one invoice.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>Invoice match checks the invoice of record, step 5.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c3" data-s="3" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">3</b><span>Manual payee check</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Bank detail changes are checked by a phone call-back.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">2</b> <b>Skipped checks.</b> Call-backs slip under deadline. Baltimore lost <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">$803K</span> to a changed vendor account.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>A rule checks every payment, step 5.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c4" data-s="4" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">4</b><span>Bank portal</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The payment is keyed again into the bank portal. A maker and a checker approve it there.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">3</b> <b>Re-keying.</b> Errors, and no single audit trail.</dd><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">4</b> <b>Blind approval.</b> The checker sees the amount, not the payee source.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>The checker sees the guard result, step 8.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c5" data-s="5" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">5</b><span>ACH or wire</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Batch ACH, or a same-day wire.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">5</b> <b>Batch rails.</b> Cut-offs, and no after-hours payouts.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>RTP and FedNow run 24/7. Network ceiling <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33" data-note="RTP and FedNow network ceilings are $10M. Each bank sets its own customer limit, often lower.">$10M</span>, bank limits vary, step 9.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-c6" data-s="6" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">6</b><span>Reconcile</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The bank statement is matched by hand in a spreadsheet.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt data-k="pain">Pain</dt><dd><b class="pdd-ar3-pn">6</b> <b>Slow close.</b> Evidence spreads across inboxes.</dd><dt>Target</dt><dd>Returns and status come back to a ledger and the ERP, step 11.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
</ol>
<ol class="pdd-ar3-steps" data-st="year1" aria-label="Nine steps in Year 1">
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y1" data-s="1" data-m="ai"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">1</b><span>Agent proposes</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The agent drafts a payout from an approved invoice. It holds no release tool.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Input</dt><dd>Invoice email and the ERP, both untrusted</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A bank detail found in a document goes to the payee guard, step 3.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y2" data-s="2" data-m="coin"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">2</b><span>Mandate check</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Inside the thin service, it checks the platform signature against the public key and the passkey mandate against the key stored at enrollment. The adapter never checks itself.</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A signing proxy adds <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21 N#22" data-note="Web Bot Auth: HTTP Message Signatures (RFC 9421) with a nonce. Visa Trusted Agent Protocol, Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use it.">Web Bot Auth</span> signatures to calls out to card networks.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y3" data-s="3" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">3</b><span>Payee guard</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span></p><p><span class="pdd-view">A rule, not a model, checks every payment.</span></p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Checks</dt><dd>Invoice match, sanctions screen, US account check</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Invoice match reads the approved invoice from the ERP and checks amount, payee and not yet paid.</li><li>The sanctions screen runs every party against the SDN list feed. A hit goes to a queue for a person.</li><li>Baltimore approved a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">changed vendor account</span> without checking it.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y4" data-s="4" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">4</b><span>Rail picker</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Picks ACH, RTP or wire before the release decision, and writes the rail into the release record.</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>RTP and FedNow network ceiling <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33" data-note="RTP raised its network limit to $10M on 9 Feb 2025. FedNow raised its network limit to $10M in Nov 2025. Each bank sets its own customer limit, often lower.">$10M</span> per payment. Bank limits vary.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y5" data-s="5" data-m="rule"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">5</b><span>Release matrix</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Payee status crossed with amount and rail. A rule releases inside the matrix, a person outside it. The release is signed and takes a one-time key.</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A retry checks the payment status first, so a timeout cannot pay twice.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y6" data-s="6" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">6</b><span>Checker app</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span></p><p>A person releases anything outside the matrix. They see the payee source and the guard result, then confirm with a passkey.</p></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y7" data-s="7" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">7</b><span>Rail adapter</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>One adapter in front of the bank API you already have. It checks the signed release before it sends.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Lock</dt><dd>An explicit deny on the adapter and the bank secret blocks every principal except the release role.</dd></dl></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y8" data-s="8" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">8</b><span>Evidence ledger</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Acknowledgments, returns and statements come back through the adapter and the event bus. Paid status goes to the ERP. A return opens an exception for a person.</p></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-y9" data-s="9" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">9</b><span>Kill switch</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Disable one agent, or switch all agent payments off. Our target is under a minute, and the adapter checks the flag on every send.</p></div></details></li>
</ol>
<ol class="pdd-ar3-steps" data-st="target" aria-label="Steps on the target payment path">
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t1" data-s="1" data-m="coin"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">1</b><span>Key registration</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The platform key is registered in a scheme directory. Only the signing proxy may sign with it. The proxy adds RFC 9421 signatures to calls out to supplier portals and card networks.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Signs</dt><dd>Signing proxy, with the platform key</dd><dt>Checks</dt><dd>The supplier portal or the card network, against the directory</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> KMS holds an Ed25519 key. The proxy runs in Lambda.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>BILL registered its Payment Agent this way for the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot, 20 Oct 2026. BILL&#39;s Payment Agent is registered through Trusted Agent Protocol.">Visa pilot</span>.</li><li>Visa Trusted Agent Protocol (TAP), Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21 N#22" data-note="Web Bot Auth: HTTP Message Signatures (RFC 9421) with a nonce. Visa Trusted Agent Protocol, Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use it.">Web Bot Auth</span>, so one proxy covers all three.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t2" data-s="2" data-m="ai"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">2</b><span>Agent and gateway</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="aws">AWS-bound</span></p><p>The agent drafts the payout from an approved invoice. Invoice PDFs, email and portals are untrusted input.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Agent runtime and tool gateway</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#14" data-note="AgentCore Runtime is a serverless hosting environment for agents and tools, with session isolation and built-in identity.">AgentCore Runtime</span> hosts the agent. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#15" data-note="AgentCore Policy checks each tool call made through AgentCore Gateway, and can require an approval before a transfer.">AgentCore Policy</span> on the Gateway denies vendor master writes and release tools.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A bank detail found in a document goes to the payee guard, step 5.</li><li>Ramp says its AP agents <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#26" data-note="Ramp Agents for AP, 7 Oct 2025: early access users flagged more than $1M of fraudulent invoices in 90 days. A vendor claim.">flagged over $1M</span> of fraudulent invoices in 90 days. <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Vendor claim">Vendor</span></li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t3" data-s="3" data-m="flow"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">3</b><span>Signed request</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>The request carries the platform signature and the signed mandate. The gateway calls with its own IAM role: Gateway role only.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Payment API</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> A public <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#12" data-note="Amazon API Gateway creates, publishes, monitors and secures REST, HTTP and WebSocket APIs.">API Gateway</span> behind an OpenAPI target. The resource policy names the gateway role.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Mandate layer: <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#15" data-note="Google AP2 v0.2 signs Intent, Cart and Payment mandates, supports payments where the human is not present, and is moving to FIDO.">Google AP2</span> and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#24" data-note="Mastercard Verifiable Intent, open-sourced and co-developed with Google, uses selective disclosure.">Verifiable Intent</span>, moving to FIDO.</li><li>A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#25" data-note="Skyfire KYAPay, an individual IETF draft. The issuer does KYC or KYB, and recipients verify against its keys.">know-your-agent token</span> names the agent, the platform and the human.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t4" data-s="4" data-m="coin"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">4</b><span>Mandate check</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Checks the platform signature against the KMS public key, Ed25519 under RFC 9421, and the passkey mandate against the key stored at enrollment. No directory round trip.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Owner</dt><dd>You, in the payment core</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#13" data-note="AWS Lambda runs code without provisioning or managing servers.">Lambda</span> checks. DynamoDB holds the keys, a nonce with a conditional write, and the revocation list.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Created and expires are enforced, and a nonce blocks replay. A key mismatch is rejected.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t5" data-s="5" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">5</b><span>Payee guard</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span></p><p><span class="pdd-view">A rule, not a model, checks every payment.</span></p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Checks</dt><dd>Invoice match, sanctions screen, US account check</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#13" data-note="AWS Lambda runs code without provisioning or managing servers.">Lambda</span> for each check, and a payee registry in DynamoDB.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Invoice match reads the approved invoice from the ERP and checks amount, payee and not yet paid.</li><li>The sanctions screen runs every party against the SDN list feed. A hit goes to a queue for a person. See <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#31">sanctions</span>.</li><li>No scheme answer means a callback for a new or changed payee, never a neutral score. Other rules: limits and a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#35 N#36" data-note="EU Verification of Payee since 9 Oct 2025. UK Confirmation of Payee covers over 99% of Faster Payments senders, Pay.UK, Jun 2025.">payee name check</span>.</li><li>Baltimore approved a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">changed vendor account</span> without checking it.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t6" data-s="6" data-m="rule"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">6</b><span>Rail and policy</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>The rail picker chooses the rail first. Then the release matrix judges payee status, amount and rail together. <span class="pdd-view">No language model sits on the approval path. The risk score can add a hold and nothing more.</span></p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Rail picker, release matrix and risk score</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> A Lambda picks the rail. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#19" data-note="Amazon Verified Permissions decides with Cedar policies and assumes the caller was authenticated elsewhere.">Verified Permissions</span> runs the matrix as Cedar rules. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#42" data-note="Amazon SageMaker AI builds, trains and deploys ML models.">SageMaker AI</span> scores. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#17" data-note="Amazon Fraud Detector stopped taking new customers on 7 Nov 2025.">Fraud Detector</span> takes no new customers.</dd></dl><div class="pdd-rm pdd-rm-dr"><p class="pdd-rm-h"><b>Release matrix</b> <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> <span class="pdd-view">Bands are your own limits. RTP and FedNow use a lower band. A risk score can send a payment to a person for release. It can never remove that step.</span></p><div class="pdd-rm-sc" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Release matrix, scroll sideways"><table class="pdd-rm-t"><thead><tr><th scope="col" rowspan="2">Payee</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">ACH</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">RTP and FedNow</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">Card token</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">Cross-border</th></tr><tr><th scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th><th scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th><th scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th><th scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><th scope="row">Established</th><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td></tr><tr><th scope="row">New</th><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td></tr><tr><th scope="row">Changed</th><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>The rail goes into the release record. If failover moves to a stricter rail, the checker approves again.</li><li>A new payee fails closed, so a person releases.</li><li>A policy change needs two approvers and a ticket.</li><li>Model risk falls under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="S#1" data-note="Fed, OCC and FDIC replaced SR 11-7 with risk-based SR 26-2 on 17 Apr 2026.">SR 26-2</span>, which replaced SR 11-7 in Apr 2026. <span class="pdd-view">Generative AI needs its own control set.</span></li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t7" data-s="7" data-m="rule"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">7</b><span>Release</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="aws">AWS-bound</span></p><p>Two exits. Inside the matrix a rule releases. Outside it the run waits for a person, step 8. Either way, release signs an assertion that the rail adapter checks.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Signs</dt><dd>Payment ID, amount, payee hash, rail, matrix cell and approver</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#23" data-note="Step Functions Standard workflows support Wait for Callback with a task token, so a run pauses until the token returns.">Step Functions</span> Standard, waiting on a task token, with the payment ID as the execution name. A DynamoDB conditional write keeps a one-time key.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>An SCP on the payment core account denies SendTaskSuccess to the agent and orchestrator roles, so neither can approve itself.</li><li>A retry checks the payment status first, so a timeout cannot pay twice.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t8" data-s="8" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">8</b><span>Person releases</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span></p><p>A checker in the checker account sees the payee source and the guard result, then approves with a passkey step-up.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Checker app</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> Cognito sign-in. The checker app assumes a release role in the payment core account to approve.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Controls modeled on <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#12" data-note="JPMorganChase plans for high agent autonomy in treasury, with controls modeled on maker-checker rules.">maker-checker</span> rules.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t9" data-s="9" data-m="bank"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">9</b><span>Bank rail</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>The adapter checks the signed release, then sends ACH or RTP through your own bank API and one controlled exit.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Lock</dt><dd>An explicit deny on the adapter and the bank secret blocks every principal except the release role.</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> KMS Verify on the signed release. Lambda adapters in private subnets across two AZs exit through a Network Firewall endpoint and a NAT gateway with an allowlisted Elastic IP. Secrets Manager holds the mTLS bank credential.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Three ways to move money. Your own bank API makes you the Originator. A sponsor bank makes a startup a TPSP under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5" data-note="Nacha fraud monitoring phase 2 took effect 19 Jun 2026 and covers Originators, TPSPs and TPSs.">Nacha phase 2</span>. A card partner issues the card.</li><li>RTP and FedNow network ceiling <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33" data-note="RTP raised its network limit to $10M on 9 Feb 2025. FedNow raised its network limit to $10M in Nov 2025. Each bank sets its own customer limit, often lower.">$10M</span> per payment. Bank limits vary.</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#13 O#14 W#17" data-note="AWS made AgentCore payments generally available on 18 Aug 2026, after a May 2026 preview: x402, MPP, and Coinbase or Stripe Privy wallets, with configurable payment limits at the infrastructure layer. AWS docs give each payment session a budget limit and an expiry, for microtransactions often under $1.">AgentCore payments</span> suits agent microtransactions, not supplier invoices.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t9b" data-s="9b" data-m="coin"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">9b</b><span>Stablecoin rail</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Off until 2027. A Cedar policy gate in the payment core, then out through the same firewall.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Opens</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31" data-note="The OCC final rule and the GENIUS Act take effect on 18 Jan 2027. Regulators missed the Jul 2026 deadline for final rules.">18 Jan 2027</span> at the earliest</dd><dt>Wins</dt><dd>Cross-border and after-hours payouts</dd><dt>Loses</dt><dd>Domestic dollars, where RTP and FedNow run 24/7</dd></dl><p class="pdd-ar3-gh">Policy gate, all four</p><ol class="pdd-vd-gates"><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">1</span><span>A coin from a permitted issuer. The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31" data-note="The OCC final rule for payment stablecoin issuers takes effect on 18 Jan 2027. It binds issuers, not payers.">OCC rule</span> binds issuers, not payers</span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">2</span><span>The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2" data-note="AML and sanctions requirements come in a separate Treasury rulemaking, still to come.">Treasury AML and sanctions rule</span>, with a wallet screen</span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">3</span><span>A corridor price win. One test found <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21" data-note="Banca d&#39;Italia sent 200 USDC across ten corridors in 2026 and found no systematic advantage over traditional channels.">no systematic edge</span></span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">4</span><span>Payee consent</span></li></ol><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#8" data-note="Ledger Insights, 1 Oct 2026: Open USD went live about 30 Sep 2026, with Coinbase, Mastercard, Shopify, Stripe and Visa as founding investors. A secondary report.">Open USD</span> went live on 30 Sep 2026.</li><li>A bank option for the same corridor: large US banks plan a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#9" data-note="The Paypers citing the WSJ, 5 Jun 2026: JPMorgan Chase, Bank of America, Citigroup, Wells Fargo and other banks, through The Clearing House, plan a shared tokenized deposit network for the first half of 2027. A secondary report.">24/7 tokenized deposit network</span> for 2027.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t10" data-s="10" data-m="flow"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">10</b><span>Card lane</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Secondary and pilot-ready. The agent handles only network tokens or single-use card references.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Owner</dt><dd>Card-issuing partner</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> A card fill Lambda in an isolated CDE subnet. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#20" data-note="Managed payment HSM functions designed for PCI PIN, PCI P2PE and PCI DSS.">Payment Cryptography</span> holds card keys. No PIN keys unless you issue cards.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Mastercard Agent Pay is live for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18" data-note="2 Jun 2026: every Mastercard issuer in Europe is enabled for Agent Pay at network level, and 36 banks completed controlled live payments with passkeys. Consumer payments.">European consumers</span> only.</li><li>The Visa B2B pilot shows <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9" data-note="No liability framework, and no disclosed volumes, participants or general availability date.">no volumes</span> yet.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t11" data-s="11" data-m="ok"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">11</b><span>Evidence ledger</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p class="pdd-ar3-bgs"><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="sox">SOX key control</span><span class="pdd-ar3-bg" data-b="port">Portable</span></p><p>Returns and status come back. A bank webhook or statement reaches the adapter, then the event bus, the ledger and the ERP. A return opens an exception for a person.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Ledger and audit store</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#36" data-note="EventBridge custom buses support retention, ordered delivery and replay.">EventBridge</span> feeds a DynamoDB ledger, then <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#22" data-note="S3 Object Lock stores objects write once, read many, assessed for SEC 17a-4, CFTC and FINRA.">S3 Object Lock</span> in compliance mode in the log archive account. An <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#40" data-note="AWS CloudTrail records actions by users, roles and services as events for auditing.">organization trail</span> logs every call.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Evidence pack: payment ID, mandate hash, key ID, guard result, matrix cell, approver and rail reference.</li><li>Agent traces carry the payment ID. Agent-originated ACH entries are tagged for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#4">Nacha monitoring</span>.</li><li>Four accounts under Organizations with SCPs: agent runtime, payment core, checker and log archive.</li></ul></div></details></li>
<li><details class="pdd-more pdd-ar-st pdd-ar3-dr" id="pdd-ar3-t12" data-s="12" data-m="risk"><summary><b class="pdd-ar-sno">12</b><span>Kill switch</span></summary><div class="pdd-ar-sb"><p>Disable one agent, switch all agent payments off, or revoke the key in the directories. Our target is under a minute.</p><dl class="pdd-ar-dl"><dt>Role</dt><dd>Control plane</dd><dt>AWS</dt><dd><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> AppConfig holds the flag, and the adapter reads it on every send. Systems Manager runs the recall playbook.</dd></dl><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Recall: an ACH <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5" data-note="A receiving bank can return a suspect credit with R17.">R17 return</span>, a wire recall, or an RTP request for return.</li></ul></div></details></li>
</ol></details>
</div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-bd-p" data-p="flow" role="region" aria-label="Flow">
<div class="pdd-bd-fhead"><p class="pdd-label">One agent-made supplier payment</p><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-bd-run">Run the payment</button><span class="pdd-bd-fst" role="status" aria-live="polite"></span></div>
<ol class="pdd-bd-steps">
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-doc"/></svg></span><b>1 Intent</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> No approved invoice behind it</p><p><i>Caught by</i> A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="The Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot ties controls to the buyer's approval.">mandate</span> bound to the invoice</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Rule</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-key"/></svg></span><b>2 Mandate check</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> Amount or payee outside the mandate</p><p><i>Caught by</i> Key signature, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#34 N#25" data-note="Mercury: the agent cannot change its card limits. KYAPay: a token names the agent, the platform and the human.">locked limits</span></p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="bank">Bank</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-shield"/></svg></span><b>3 Payee guard</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> Bank details changed or name mismatch</p><p><i>Caught by</i> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#35 N#36">Name check</span>, then a callback</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="person">Rule, person</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-risk"/></svg></span><b>4 AI risk score</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> An <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#7" data-note="74% of firms were hit by email compromise in 2025, AFP reports.">email compromise</span> pattern slips by</p><p><i>Caught by</i> <span class="pdd-view">The risk score can add a hold and nothing more</span></p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="ai">AI</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-matrix"/></svg></span><b>5 Release matrix</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> The agent releases alone</p><p><i>Caught by</i> A rule for known payees in band, a person for the rest, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#12 N#10">maker-checker</span></p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="person">Rule or person</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-rails"/></svg></span><b>6 Send</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> Sent twice, or over the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37" data-note="RTP network ceiling $10M per payment since 9 Feb 2025. Bank limits vary.">rail limit</span></p><p><i>Caught by</i> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#29">Idempotency key</span>, rail limits and value date</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Rule</span></li>
<li class="pdd-bd-step" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-sh"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-ledger"/></svg></span><b>7 Evidence</b></div><p><i>Fails</i> No proof of what was approved</p><p><i>Caught by</i> A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#24">signed record</span> of the approval</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Rule</span></li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="pdd-bd-p" data-p="plan" role="region" aria-label="Delivery">
<p class="pdd-bd-ptag"><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span></p>
<ol class="pdd-bd-phases">
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-ph" data-m="risk"><p class="pdd-label">Weeks 0 to 6</p><h3 class="pdd-bd-pn">Guard and mandates</h3><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Outcomes</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Payee guard on every channel</li><li>One signed mandate per payment</li><li>Agents read bank data only</li></ul><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Controls</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Limits the agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#34">cannot change</span></li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#35 N#36">Payee name check</span> where a scheme exists</li><li>A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#4">Nacha monitoring</span> record</li></ul></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-ph" data-m="rule"><p class="pdd-label">Weeks 6 to 12</p><h3 class="pdd-bd-pn">Agent sandbox</h3><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Outcomes</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Agent drafts real payments in shadow</li><li>AI scores checked against people</li><li>One rail, one entity, one bank</li></ul><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Controls</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#12">person releases</span> every new payee</li><li>Agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21" data-note="Web Bot Auth signatures, Cloudflare, Oct 2025.">signs every request</span></li><li>Agent limits in the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">security procedure</span></li></ul></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-ph" data-m="ok"><p class="pdd-label">Week 12 onward</p><h3 class="pdd-bd-pn">Scaled rails</h3><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Outcomes</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Add <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37">RTP</span> and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#20 N#18">card agent tokens</span></li><li>Low-risk payments release by rule</li><li>An evidence pack per payment</li></ul><p class="pdd-bd-pl">Controls</p><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>A person releases outside the matrix</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#38">Liability terms</span> per rail</li><li>Quarterly review of mandates</li></ul></li>
</ol>
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-apc" data-m="ok" role="group" aria-labelledby="pdd-apc-h"><div class="pdd-apc-head"><h3 class="pdd-apc-t" id="pdd-apc-h">Your AP desk</h3><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span><p class="pdd-apc-say">Our arithmetic. Cross-border always goes to a person.</p></div><ul class="pdd-apc-out" aria-live="polite"><li data-m="ok"><b class="pdd-num" data-apc="save">$240K</b><span>yearly saving</span></li><li data-m="flow"><b class="pdd-num" data-apc="back">21</b><span>payback month</span></li><li data-m="rule"><b class="pdd-num" data-apc="hrs">15.8</b><span>checker hours a day</span></li><li data-m="bank"><b class="pdd-num" data-apc="stp">45%</b><span>straight through</span></li></ul><div class="pdd-apc-mix" role="img" aria-label="Payment mix: 65% ACH, 10% card, 25% cross-border. 45% released by rule."><span class="pdd-apc-seg" data-m="bank" data-seg="ach" style="--w:65%"></span><span class="pdd-apc-seg" data-m="flow" data-seg="card" style="--w:10%"></span><span class="pdd-apc-seg" data-m="coin" data-seg="cross" style="--w:25%"></span><i class="pdd-apc-stp" style="--w:45%"></i></div><p class="pdd-apc-key"><span data-m="bank"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>ACH <b data-apc="ach">65%</b></span><span data-m="flow"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Card <b data-apc="card">10%</b></span><span data-m="coin"><i class="pdd-dot"></i>Cross-border <b data-apc="cross">25%</b></span><span class="pdd-apc-kl">Line: released by rule</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-apc-in"><summary>Your inputs</summary><p class="pdd-apc-how">Illustration values, except cost per payment at the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#18" data-note="WEX restates a 2025 Ardent Partners benchmark: $9.40 to process an average invoice, $2.78 best in class. Applied per payment as an illustration.">WEX average</span>. Saving counts that cost on payments released by rule, less run cost and fraud loss. 250 working days.</p><div class="pdd-apc-grid"><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-sup">Suppliers</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-sup">3,000</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-sup" data-k="sup" min="500" max="20000" step="100" value="3000"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-pay">Payments a year</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-pay">72,000</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-pay" data-k="pay" min="6000" max="600000" step="1000" value="72000"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="bank"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-ach">ACH share</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-ach">65%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-ach" data-k="ach" min="0" max="100" step="5" value="65"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="flow"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-card">Card share</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-card">10%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-card" data-k="card" min="0" max="100" step="5" value="10"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="cost"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-cost">Cost per payment</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-cost">$9.40</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-cost" data-k="cost" min="2.78" max="10.89" step="0.01" value="9.4"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="ok"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-rule">Released by rule</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-rule">60%</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-rule" data-k="rule" min="0" max="100" step="5" value="60"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="rule"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-min">Minutes per hold</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-min">6</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-min" data-k="min" min="1" max="30" step="1" value="6"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="rule"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-build">Build cost</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-build">$400K</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-build" data-k="build" min="50000" max="2000000" step="25000" value="400000"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="rule"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-run">AWS run a year, our arithmetic</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-run">$15K</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-run" data-k="run" min="5000" max="500000" step="5000" value="15000"></div><div class="pdd-s4-sl pdd-apc-sl" data-m="risk"><p class="pdd-s4-lab"><label for="pdd-apc-loss">Fraud loss a year</label></p><output for="pdd-apc-loss">$50K</output><input type="range" id="pdd-apc-loss" data-k="loss" min="0" max="500000" step="5000" value="50000"></div></div></details></div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-bd-qs" data-open-for="buyer"><summary>Ask your bank</summary><ol class="pdd-bd-ql"><li>Will you let our agent start a payment, or <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">read only</span>?</li><li>How do agent limits enter our <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">security procedure agreement</span>?</li><li>Do you issue <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#19 N#18">agent tokens</span> on Visa or Mastercard?</li><li>Who carries the loss when an agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10 N#11" data-note="BofA is working out who is liable when an AI authorizes a payment. JPMorgan's CFO said customers who share credentials with agents could be responsible.">pays the wrong supplier</span>?</li><li>How does your <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5">Nacha fraud monitoring</span> treat our agent's payments?</li><li>As an Originator, how do we meet our own <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5">monitoring duty</span>?</li><li>Does our insurance cover agent-initiated transfers? Ask the insurer too.</li></ol></details>
</div>

<article class="pdd-panel pdd-vd-call" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-vd-ch"><svg class="pdd-vd-ico pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-policy"/></svg><div><p class="pdd-label">The call</p><h3 class="pdd-vd-h pdd-view">Year one: a signed agent identity, plus ACH and RTP through your bank API.</h3><p class="pdd-vd-sub">The card lane is pilot-ready. A release matrix decides who releases.</p></div></div><ul class="pdd-vd-fix"><li data-m="risk">A rule releases known payees in band. A person releases the rest.</li><li data-m="rule">No language model sits on the approval path. The risk score can add a hold and nothing more.</li><li data-m="coin">The stablecoin rail, step 9b, stays off until its policy gate clears.</li></ul><div class="pdd-rm pdd-vd-rm"><p class="pdd-rm-h"><b>Release matrix</b> <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative">Illustrative</span> <span class="pdd-view">Bands are your own limits. RTP and FedNow use a lower band. A risk score can send a payment to a person for release. It can never remove that step.</span></p><div class="pdd-rm-sc" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Release matrix, scroll sideways"><table class="pdd-rm-t"><thead><tr><th scope="col" rowspan="2">Payee</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">ACH</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">RTP and FedNow</th><th scope="colgroup" colspan="2">Card token</th><th scope="colgroup" scope="col">Low</th><th scope="col">High</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><th scope="row">Established</th><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="rule">Rule</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td><td><i class="pdd-rm-c" data-r="person">Person</i></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div><div class="pdd-vd-drs"><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>Protocol stack</summary><ol class="pdd-ps"><li data-ly="id"><b>Identity</b><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21 N#22" data-note="Web Bot Auth: HTTP Message Signatures (RFC 9421) with a nonce. Visa Trusted Agent Protocol, Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use it.">Web Bot Auth</span> over RFC 9421. Visa Trusted Agent Protocol (TAP), Mastercard Agent Pay and Amex use it.</span></li><li data-ly="man"><b>Mandate</b><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#15" data-note="Google AP2 v0.2 signs Intent, Cart and Payment mandates and is moving to FIDO.">Google AP2</span> and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#24 W#16" data-note="Mastercard Verifiable Intent, 5 Mar 2026, co-developed with Google per Mastercard. Published as Draft v0.1 under Apache 2.0 on its own site.">Verifiable Intent</span>, via FIDO.</span></li><li data-ly="rail"><b>Rail</b><span>Your bank API for ACH and RTP, a card token, or <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="S#2" data-note="MPP, maintained by Tempo and Stripe, lists Tempo, Stripe, Lightning and Solana methods. No ACH method, so no protocol claim for the bank rail.">MPP</span> and x402 for machine payments.</span></li></ol><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li><b>Signs:</b> your agent platform, with a key in the Visa Agentic Directory, as <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="BILL&#39;s Payment Agent is registered through Trusted Agent Protocol for the Visa pilot of 20 Oct 2026.">BILL did</span>. <b>Verifies:</b> the supplier portal or your bank edge.</li><li>Mastercard Agent Pay is live for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18">European consumers</span> only, on vendor figures. The Visa B2B pilot has <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9">no volumes</span> yet.</li><li>AWS WAF can <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#18">pre-check</span> signatures at the edge. OpenAI ACP is <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#16">still in beta</span>.</li></ul></details><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>Why the guard</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">UCC 4A-202</span> the payer is bound when the bank followed the agreed procedure.</li><li>Baltimore approved a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">changed vendor account</span> without checking it.</li><li>Nacha now requires monitoring for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5">false pretenses</span>.</li><li>AgentCore Policy can require an <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#15">approval before a transfer</span>.</li><li>Stripe agent tools need a person to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#7" data-note="Stripe MCP docs: human confirmation before certain agent write actions such as refunds and outbound payments. From 30 Nov 2026 the MCP server takes agent API keys only, so full-access keys and restricted keys without the Agent tag stop working.">confirm refunds and payouts</span>. Plain keys stop working on 30 Nov 2026.</li></ul></details><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>What changes it</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Visa or Mastercard publish agent liability rules that give mandates weight. Move the card lane from pilot to primary. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4 N#9" data-note="Dispute rules say little about agents today, and the Visa B2B pilot sets no liability framework.">Evidence</span></li><li><span class="pdd-view">The dispute pilot shows mandates carry no weight. Switch to a protocol hub.</span></li><li>The Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot shows volumes or reaches general release. Put the supplier portal path first. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9">Pilot</span></li><li>Big banks let outside agents move money. Add a bank API agent channel. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">Today</span></li><li>All four policy gates clear: permitted issuer, Treasury AML and sanctions rule, a corridor price win and payee consent. Switch on the stablecoin rail, step 9b. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31">Gates</span></li><li>UK HM Treasury or the EU PSR assigns liability for agent payments. Rerun the loss table. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#6 O#5" data-note="HM Treasury asked in Jul 2026 whether liability rules need updating for agentic payments. The EU PSR deal is provisional.">Consultations</span></li><li>OpenAI ACP ships a stable release with a major B2B adopter. Add it at the mandate layer. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#16">Beta today</span></li></ul></details><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>Runners-up</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>JPMorganChase, runner-up: <span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-tag="spec" data-src="N#12" data-note="Plan. JPMorganChase plans maker-checker controls for agents in treasury, Oct 2026.">Spec</span> agent controls, a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#12" data-note="J.P. Morgan Payments and Mirakl signed a global agreement on 10 Mar 2026 for agentic commerce payments, tokens and fraud protection.">Mirakl deal</span>, and it <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">blocks outside agents</span>.</li><li>Skyfire, to watch: <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#25">KYAPay</span> is an individual IETF draft.</li><li>Six banks, Bank of America and NatWest among them, set <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#4" data-note="Bank of America, 22 Sep 2026: ASB, Bank of America, Capital One, Commonwealth Bank of Australia, ING and NatWest published joint principles for trusted agentic commerce. No liability allocation.">agent commerce principles</span> on 22 Sep 2026. None assigns liability yet.</li><li>Ramp's Visa agents have no launch date. Natural names no customers. Rain gives no date or volumes.</li></ul></details></div></article>
<article class="pdd-vd-v pdd-panel pdd-vd-path" data-v="path" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-vd-tt"><div class="pdd-vd-tc" data-m="risk"><p class="pdd-vd-th">Today</p><ul><li>Payee changes checked by phone call-back</li><li>Approvals keyed into a bank portal</li><li>Batch ACH and wire, with cut-offs</li></ul><p class="pdd-vd-tf"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#7" data-note="AFP 2026 survey: 74% of firms were hit by email compromise in 2025.">74%</span> of firms hit by email compromise in 2025</p></div><div class="pdd-vd-tc" data-m="ok"><p class="pdd-vd-th">Target 2027</p><ul><li><span class="pdd-view">An agent proposes. The release matrix picks a rule or a person.</span></li><li>ACH and RTP first, card tokens next</li><li>The stablecoin rail, step 9b, once its policy gate clears</li></ul><p class="pdd-vd-tf"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#8" data-note="Ledger Insights, 1 Oct 2026: Open USD went live about 30 Sep 2026, with Coinbase, Mastercard, Shopify, Stripe and Visa as founding investors. A secondary report.">Open USD</span> live since 30 Sep 2026</p></div><div class="pdd-vd-tc pdd-vd-chg" data-m="flow"><p class="pdd-vd-th">What changes</p><dl><dt>Who acts</dt><dd><span class="pdd-vd-was">A clerk keys it</span><span class="pdd-vd-now">The agent proposes</span></dd><dt>Who checks</dt><dd><span class="pdd-vd-was">Sees the amount</span><span class="pdd-vd-now">Sees the payee source</span></dd><dt>Rail</dt><dd><span class="pdd-vd-was">Batch ACH or wire</span><span class="pdd-vd-now">Several rails, 24/7</span></dd><dt>Evidence</dt><dd><span class="pdd-vd-was">Inboxes, spreadsheets</span><span class="pdd-vd-now">A write-once record</span></dd></dl></div></div><div class="pdd-vd-pr"><ol class="pdd-vd-p3"><li data-m="risk"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">1</span><span><b>Now</b> Identity layer, payee guard, ACH and RTP</span></li><li data-m="coin"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">2</span><span><b>From <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31">18 Jan 2027</span></b> Stablecoin rail pilot on one corridor, if all four gates clear</span></li><li data-m="ok"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">3</span><span><b>Scale</b> Card lane. Rules release more known payees</span></li></ol><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-vd-go" data-go="target">Target design</button></div></article>
<div class="pdd-vd-v pdd-vd-pl" data-v="players">
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-vd-col" data-m="bank"><p class="pdd-vd-hd"><svg class="pdd-ar-i pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg><b>Enterprise</b></p><ol class="pdd-vd-ps"><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">1</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Visa</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa, BILL and Billtrust, 20 Oct 2026: AI agents pay approved buyer invoices end to end through supplier portals. In pilot with partners including AWS.">on-ramp</span>.</p></div></li><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">2</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Mastercard</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-src="N#18" data-note="2 Jun 2026: Mastercard says every issuer in Europe is enabled for Agent Pay at network level, and 36 banks completed controlled live payments with passkeys. Consumer payments, vendor figures.">Vendor</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw">Mastercard Agent Pay on every European issuer, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18">36 banks</span> live. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#24 W#16" data-note="Mastercard Verifiable Intent, 5 Mar 2026, co-developed with Google per Mastercard. Published as Draft v0.1 under Apache 2.0 on its own site.">Verifiable Intent</span> is open, draft v0.1.</p></div></li><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">3</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Stripe</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-src="H#21" data-note="Stripe Sessions, 29 Apr 2026: Stripe says MPP agent payments are accepted in stablecoins and in fiat. Issuing for agents, single-use virtual cards, was previewed at Sessions on 29 Apr 2026.">Spec</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#1" data-note="Stripe Shared Payment Tokens carry limits for currency, maximum amount and expiry, and the agent can revoke them.">Tokens</span> capped by amount and expiry. MPP in stablecoin and fiat.</p></div></li></ol></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-vd-col" data-m="flow"><p class="pdd-vd-hd"><svg class="pdd-ar-i pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-plug"/></svg><b>Startups</b></p><ol class="pdd-vd-ps"><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">1</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Ramp</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-src="O#26" data-note="Ramp Agents for AP, launched 7 Oct 2025: code invoices, recommend approvals, pay by card in vendor portals and check for fraud.
