Reported 2 Apr 2026: Ramp plans AI agents for corporate bill pay on Visa Intelligent Commerce and Trusted Agent Protocol. No launch date.">Pilot</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw">AP agents code, approve and pay invoices. Visa agents planned.</p></div></li><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">2</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Natural</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-src="O#27" data-note="21 Jul 2026: Natural says six products are generally available, Wallets (FDIC-insured accounts for agents), Vaults, Pay, Request, Transfer and Connect. Raised a $30M Series A. Names no customers.">Vendor</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#27">Six products</span> live, with FDIC-insured agent wallets. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#27">$30M</span> raised.</p></div></li><li class="pdd-vd-p"><span class="pdd-vd-rk">3</span><div><p class="pdd-vd-pn"><b>Rain</b><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-src="O#28" data-note="Rain says agents have used its scoped virtual cards in production for months. The Agent Control Layer is in beta. The page has no date or volumes.">Vendor</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-tag="spec" data-src="O#28" data-note="Beta. Agent Control Layer, beta: agent and program limits enforced at card issue and at transfer start.">Spec</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw">Stablecoin-funded scoped cards. An alliance of <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#29 R#3" data-note="Rain launched the Agentic Payments Alliance on 18 Aug 2026 with 26 founding members, including Visa, Mastercard, Fiserv and Circle.">26 founders</span>, Visa and Mastercard among them.</p></div></li></ol></article>
<p class="pdd-vd-pw pdd-vd-open"><b>Open lanes</b> <span class="pdd-view">US payee validation, a cross-network mandate evidence pack, mid-market ERP connectors and supplier-side agent verification.</span></p>
</div>
<article class="pdd-vd-v pdd-panel pdd-vd-coin" data-v="coin" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-vd-cl"><p class="pdd-vd-hd"><svg class="pdd-ar-i pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-coin"/></svg><b>Stablecoin rail</b></p><p class="pdd-vd-pw"><span class="pdd-view">Optional, behind the rail picker.</span> The same payee guard plus wallet screening. Off until all four gates clear.</p><ol class="pdd-vd-gates"><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">1</span><span>A coin from a permitted issuer. The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 O#31" data-note="The OCC final rule for payment stablecoin issuers takes effect on 18 Jan 2027. It binds issuers, not payers.">OCC rule</span> binds issuers, not payers</span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">2</span><span>The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2" data-note="AML and sanctions requirements come in a separate Treasury rulemaking, still to come.">Treasury AML and sanctions rule</span></span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">3</span><span>A corridor price win. One test found <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21" data-note="Banca d&#39;Italia sent 200 USDC across ten corridors in 2026 and found no systematic advantage over traditional channels.">no systematic edge</span></span></li><li><span class="pdd-vd-jn">4</span><span>Payee consent</span></li></ol></div><div class="pdd-vd-cr"><div class="pdd-vd-wl"><div><p class="pdd-vd-wh" data-k="win">Wins</p><ul><li>Cross-border payouts, weekends included, to suppliers who take USDC</li><li>Agent payments under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#14" data-note="AWS docs describe AgentCore payments for agent microtransactions, often under $1, with a budget and an expiry per session.">$1</span></li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#6" data-note="The Block, 10 Jun 2026: a layer on the Mastercard network across cards, accounts and stablecoins, with spending limits enforced in code.">Mastercard Agent Pay for Machines</span> runs on cards, accounts and stablecoins</li><li>Spend limits written into the payment <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#13 O#28" data-note="AgentCore payments enforces payment limits at the infrastructure layer. Rain enforces limits at card issue and transfer start.">itself</span></li></ul></div><div><p class="pdd-vd-wh" data-k="lose">Does not win</p><ul><li>Domestic dollars: RTP and FedNow run 24/7, network ceiling <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33" data-note="Network ceilings. Each bank sets its own customer limit, often lower.">$10M</span>, bank limits vary</li><li>High value: Fedwire averages <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#10" data-note="Fedwire Funds moved about $1.148 quadrillion across 217.3 million transfers in 2025.">$5.28M</span></li><li>Thin demand: the market sits near <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#1" data-note="FSB, 12 Oct 2026: stablecoin market value around $300 billion, essentially unchanged from a year earlier.">$300B</span></li><li>Suppliers who want fiat. Large US banks plan a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="R#9" data-note="The Paypers citing the WSJ, 5 Jun 2026: JPMorgan Chase, Bank of America, Citigroup, Wells Fargo and other banks, through The Clearing House, plan a shared tokenized deposit network for the first half of 2027.">24/7 tokenized deposit network</span> for 2027</li></ul></div></div><details class="pdd-more pdd-vd-dr"><summary>Volumes, with care</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Visa's <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#30" data-note="Visa, 8 Sep 2026: stablecoin settlement passed a $20B annualized run rate. That is card settlement.">$20B run rate</span> is card settlement, not supplier payouts.</li><li>x402 settled <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="AP#1 AP#2" data-note="One tracker, October 2026, raw counts that include tests. An independent screen puts the agent share at 0.6% to 7.5%.">about $274K</span> in October 2026, and about 4,000 wallets make up <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#2" data-note="Reported 21 Apr 2026: about 4,000 wallets drive roughly 90% of adjusted x402 spending.">roughly 90%</span> of spend.</li><li>ACH carried <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#2" data-note="Nacha: ACH payments in 2025 were worth $93 trillion, with close to 8.1 billion B2B payments.">$93T</span> in 2025, including 8.1B B2B payments.</li></ul></details></div></article>
<article class="pdd-vd-v pdd-panel pdd-vd-jobs" data-v="jobs" data-m="ai"><p class="pdd-vd-hd"><svg class="pdd-ar-i pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-agent"/></svg><b>Six jobs for the payment agent</b></p><ol class="pdd-vd-js"><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">1</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#15 O#1" data-note="AP2 signs mandates. Stripe tokens carry amount, currency and expiry limits.">Intent capture</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Drafts the mandate: who, cap, expiry</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="ai">Agent drafts, person signs</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">2</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#31 O#26" data-note="Oracle and Ramp agents match and code invoices. Vendor claims.">Invoice match</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Codes the invoice and runs the match</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="ai">Agent does</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">3</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5 O#26 N#35 N#36" data-note="Nacha false-pretenses monitoring, Ramp fraud checks, payee name checks in the EU and UK.">Payee risk flag</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">New payee, changed bank details, name mismatch</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="person">Agent flags, release held</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">4</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#37 O#33" data-note="RTP and FedNow network ceilings are $10M per payment. Bank limits vary.">Rail choice</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Limits, cut-offs, cost and value date</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Rule decides</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">5</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#32 N#31" data-note="Coupa batch agents and Oracle payment tracking. Vendor claims.">Exceptions</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Batches, matches and chases</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="ai">Agent does, person clears</span></li><li class="pdd-vd-j"><p class="pdd-vd-jh"><span class="pdd-vd-jn">6</span><span>Pay date</span></p><p class="pdd-vd-jw">Due date against an early-pay discount</p><span class="pdd-bd-a" data-a="rule">Agent proposes, rule checks</span></li></ol><p class="pdd-vd-pw pdd-vd-jf">The release matrix sends a new or changed payee, or a high band, to a <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#12 O#15 O#23" data-note="Maker-checker controls at JPMorganChase. AgentCore Policy can require an approval. Step Functions can wait for the checker.">checker</span>.</p></article>
</div>
<div class="pdd-bd-drs">
<details class="pdd-more pdd-bd-dr" data-p="who" id="pdd-bd-who"><summary>Who gains</summary>
<div class="pdd-bd-cards">
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Bank</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18" data-note="Mastercard says every issuer in Europe is enabled for Agent Pay at network level, and 36 banks ran live transactions with passkeys.">36</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">banks in controlled consumer pilots, Europe</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Vendor figure">Vendor</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Issues and verifies <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#19" data-note="Visa Agentic Ready: 20+ partners live in the UK and EU, 85+ planned in APAC and LatAm. Issuers test tokenization and authorization.">agent tokens</span>, and stays in every approval.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>Still <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#38" data-note="A fraud vendor's opinion: issuers still retain liability and must show why they approved or declined an agent transaction.">carries the liability</span> and must explain each decision.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd>Scores <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#39" data-note="FIS says its issuer platform uses know-your-agent data to authorize agent payments. A vendor claim.">know-your-agent data</span> when it authorizes.</dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-erp"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Business payer</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#7" data-note="AFP 2026 survey: 76% of firms had payments fraud in 2025, 74% were hit by email compromise, and 17% use AI against fraud.">74%</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">of firms hit by email compromise, 2025</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Payment runs without keying, as <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#32 W#7" data-note="Coupa says its payment batch agent ran 2,395 payments worth $20.1M in 5 weeks at one customer. A vendor claim. The agent prepares the batch and does not have the final say, a person releases it.">Coupa reports</span>. A person still releases.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>The loss stays with the payer if the bank <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6" data-note="UCC 4A-202(b): an order counts as the customer's whether or not authorized, if the agreed security procedure was commercially reasonable and the bank followed it in good faith.">followed the procedure</span>.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd>Matches invoices, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#31" data-note="Oracle Payables and Payments agents cover intake, matching, fraud checks, early pay, cards and bank execution. Oracle, Oct 2025. A vendor claim.">checks fraud</span> and proposes the run.</dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-person"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Supplier</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Billtrust reports 20M+ buyers and $1 trillion in annual trade credit volume. Its own figure.">$1T</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">annual trade credit volume on Billtrust</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Vendor figure">Vendor</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Paid through its portal with <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot, 20 Oct 2026: agents pay approved invoices through supplier portals.">no CAPTCHA or shared logins</span>.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>Fraudsters pose as the supplier and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5" data-note="Nacha's False Pretenses covers email compromise, vendor impersonation and payroll impersonation.">redirect</span> its money.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd>Checks the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#22" data-note="Trusted Agent Protocol: the merchant checks the agent's RFC 9421 signature against the agent's public key.">agent's signature</span> before it accepts.</dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Network or processor</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#19" data-note="Visa Agentic Ready: 20+ partners live in the UK and EU, 85+ planned in APAC and LatAm.">20+</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">Visa partners live in the UK and EU</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Vendor figure">Vendor</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Runs the agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#21" data-note="Visa TAP and Mastercard Agent Pay both use Web Bot Auth, with key directories run by Visa and Mastercard. Cloudflare, Oct 2025.">key directory</span> and the token layer.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>The Visa B2B pilot has <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9" data-note="No liability framework, and no disclosed volumes, participants or general availability date.">no liability framework</span>.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd>Binds a token to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#18" data-note="Mastercard Agent Pay issues agentic tokens for each agent.">each agent</span> and checks its use.</dd></dl></article>
<article class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-card" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-agent"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Agent platform</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="BILL serves about 500,000 businesses. Its own figure.">500K</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">businesses on BILL</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-note="Vendor figure">Vendor</span></p><dl class="pdd-bd-dl"><dt>Gains</dt><dd>Moves from automating payables to <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa calls it the first live pilot through supplier portals, 20 Oct 2026. Visa&#39;s own claim.">executing</span> the payments.</dd><dt>Risk</dt><dd>Large banks <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10" data-note="JPMorgan, Citi, BofA and Schwab will not let outside agents move money. JPMorgan allows read-only access through Plaid. The report covers consumer accounts.">block outside agents</span> on consumer accounts.</dd><dt>AI role</dt><dd><span class="pdd-view">Runs the payment inside the mandate.</span></dd></dl></article>
</div>
</details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-bd-dr" data-p="loss" id="pdd-bd-loss"><summary>Who loses</summary>
<div class="pdd-wl">
<div class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-heat"><div class="pdd-wl-hh"><p class="pdd-label pdd-view">Money at risk against power to fix, our scores from 1 to 5</p><p class="pdd-wl-say">The payer loses most. The bank can fix most.</p><p class="pdd-wl-key"><i data-k="risk"></i>Money at risk <i data-k="fix"></i>Power to fix</p></div><div class="pdd-wl-grid" role="group" aria-label="Pick a party to highlight"><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="payer" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Business payer: money at risk 5 of 5, power to fix 3 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Payer</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:5"><i></i><b>5</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:3"><i></i><b>3</b></span></button><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="supplier" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Supplier: money at risk 4 of 5, power to fix 1 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Supplier</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:4"><i></i><b>4</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:1"><i></i><b>1</b></span></button><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="bank" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Bank: money at risk 3 of 5, power to fix 5 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Bank</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:3"><i></i><b>3</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:5"><i></i><b>5</b></span></button><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="network" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Network or processor: money at risk 2 of 5, power to fix 4 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Network</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:2"><i></i><b>2</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:4"><i></i><b>4</b></span></button><button type="button" class="pdd-wl-col" data-party="platform" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Agent platform: money at risk 1 of 5, power to fix 4 of 5"><span class="pdd-wl-nm">Platform</span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="risk" style="--v:1"><i></i><b>1</b></span><span class="pdd-wl-bar" data-k="fix" style="--v:4"><i></i><b>4</b></span></button></div></div>
<ol class="pdd-wl-cards"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="payer" data-m="cost"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">1</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-erp"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Business payer</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="L#1" data-note="FBI IC3 2025: business email compromise losses reported, all channels.">$3.05B</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">email fraud losses, 2025</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Owns the payee master data and the mandate it signs. Only it knows the real supplier.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Bound under <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">UCC 4A-202</span> when the bank followed procedure.</li><li>Courts can make it <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#32" data-note="Foley Hoag, 3 Apr 2026: most courts put BEC losses on the party best placed to prevent the fraud. In one 2024 case the payer still owed the agreed amount.">pay the supplier again</span>.</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#7">74%</span> of organizations were hit by email fraud in 2025.</li><li>Losses: <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#7" data-note="Children's Healthcare of Atlanta, Jun 2023: a hacker posing as a vendor changed its ACH instructions.">$5.3M</span> at a children's hospital group, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="K#1">$803K</span> never recovered in Baltimore, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#9" data-note="Arup, Jan 2024: a Hong Kong employee joined a video call with deepfaked colleagues.">$25.6M</span> at Arup.</li><li>The UK CMA says a business answers for its agent as for an <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#3" data-note="Reported guidance of 9 Mar 2026.">employee</span>. Customers who share credentials with agents <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#11">could be responsible</span>.</li></ul></details></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="supplier" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">2</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-person"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Supplier</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4" data-note="Chargeflow cites a Mastercard article with Datos Insights research: merchant chargeback costs of $36.9 billion in 2026, rising to $46.1 billion in 2029. A forecast.">$36.9B</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">merchant chargebacks, 2026 forecast</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Has little power to fix it. Gains when banks check <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#35 N#36">payee names</span>.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>In card flows the merchant <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4">generally carries</span> chargebacks.</li><li>In push payments it goes unpaid while <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#32">courts split</span>.</li><li>One firm says <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#8" data-note="Frontline Fabrics sued its insurer and banks on 26 Dec 2025. The claims are allegations only.">$1.43M</span> meant for its supplier went to a new account. An allegation.</li></ul></details></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="bank" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">3</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Bank</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#4 R#10 R#11" data-note="UK mandatory APP fraud reimbursement since 7 Oct 2024 covers consumers, micro firms and small charities. The PSR cap per claim converted at the Bank of England rate of 22 Oct 2026. Larger firms fall outside it.">$114K</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">about $114,000 (UK cap) per refund claim</span><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-note="Our arithmetic">Illustrative</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Owns the security procedure and the duty to monitor.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Carries the loss when its procedure was not <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">commercially reasonable</span> or not followed.</li><li>Must run Nacha <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#4 N#5">fraud monitoring</span>.</li><li>Gets <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#8">sued</span> when a payment goes astray.</li><li>Large banks <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">block outside agents</span> on consumer accounts.</li></ul></details></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="network" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">4</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Network or processor</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#11" data-note="Amex Agent Purchase Protection, Apr 2026: covers agent errors when the agent is registered and the customer's intent is authenticated.">Amex</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">covers registered agent errors</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Owns the dispute rules and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#14 N#23">agent registration</span>.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>Merchants and issuers carry <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="M#4 N#38">most losses</span>.</li><li>Amex covers agent errors for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#11">registered agents</span>.</li></ul></details></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-wl-card" data-party="platform" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-head"><span class="pdd-wl-rk">5</span><svg class="pdd-bd-icon pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-agent"/></svg><h3 class="pdd-bd-name">Agent platform</h3></div><p class="pdd-bd-fig"><span class="pdd-num pdd-bd-num"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9" data-note="The Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot sets no formal liability framework.">None</span></span><span class="pdd-bd-cap">liability rule for agents yet</span></p><p class="pdd-wl-fx"><b>Who fixes</b> <span class="pdd-view">Owns the mandate scope: <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#1 N#34 O#28 O#14">caps, expiry and revocation</span>.</span></p><details class="pdd-more pdd-wl-ev"><summary>Evidence</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-ul"><li>The Visa B2B pilot sets <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#9">no liability framework</span>.</li><li><span class="pdd-view">No rule yet assigns fault to an agent.</span></li></ul></details></li></ol>
</div>
</details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-bd-dr" data-p="int" id="pdd-bd-int"><summary>Integrations</summary>
<p class="pdd-bd-ilead"><b>Three layers.</b> <span class="pdd-bd-ly" data-ly="id">Identity</span><span class="pdd-bd-ly" data-ly="man">Mandate</span><span class="pdd-bd-ly" data-ly="rail">Rail</span><span class="pdd-bd-ly" data-ly="ctl">Controls</span> <span class="pdd-view">Year one: identity, plus ACH and RTP through the bank API.</span></p>
<ul class="pdd-bd-tiles">
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="ctl" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-erp"/></svg></span><b>ERP and AP</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-src="N#31 N#32 N#8 W#7" data-note="Oracle (Oct 2025), Coupa and BILL agents prepare payment batches. Coupa: 2,395 payments worth $20.1M in 5 weeks at one customer, and a person releases. Vendor claims.">Vendor</span> Oracle, Coupa and BILL agents prepare batches. A person releases.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="rail" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-rails"/></svg></span><b>Bank rails</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="N#37 N#4" data-note="RTP network ceiling $10M since 9 Feb 2025, bank limits vary. Nacha fraud monitoring phase 2 took effect 19 Jun 2026.">Live</span> ACH and RTP through your bank API, year one. RTP network ceiling $10M, bank limits vary.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="rail" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg></span><b>Bank data access</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="N#10" data-note="Read only. JPMorgan allows read-only agent access through Plaid. The report covers consumer accounts. No bank-branded treasury API for agent payments was found.">Pilot</span> Read only. Large banks do not let agents pay on consumer accounts.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="man" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-agent"/></svg></span><b>Google AP2</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-tag="spec" data-src="N#15 N#14" data-note="AP2 v0.2. Standardization moves to FIDO, where Mastercard and Visa chair the Payments group.">Spec</span> Signed mandates with Verifiable Intent, moving to FIDO.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="man" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-card"/></svg></span><b>OpenAI ACP</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-tag="spec" data-src="N#16 N#17 M#9" data-note="Latest stable spec dated 17 Apr 2026, labeled beta, with a Delegate Payment spec. In Mar 2026 ChatGPT moved checkout to retailer sites.">Spec</span> Beta, with a Delegate Payment flow.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="id" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg></span><b>Visa B2B pilot</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="N#8 N#20 N#9" data-note="Live but controlled for consumers. The B2B pilot with BILL and Billtrust started 20 Oct 2026 and discloses no volumes. Ramp uses it for bill pay.">Pilot</span> B2B with BILL and Billtrust, no volumes yet.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="id" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-card"/></svg></span><b>Mastercard Agent Pay</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="vendor" data-tag="vendor" data-src="N#18" data-note="Every European issuer enabled, 36 banks live with passkeys. Vendor figures. No B2B release found.">Vendor</span> Consumer payments in Europe, vendor figures. No B2B release.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="rail" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-plug"/></svg></span><b>x402 and MPP</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="AP#1 S#2" data-note="One tracker, Oct 2026: about 5.1M transactions and $274K, down from 17.9M transactions in August. Raw counts include tests. MPP, maintained by Tempo and Stripe, lists Tempo, Stripe, Lightning and Solana methods. No ACH method.">Live</span> x402 settled about $274K in October 2026. MPP covers machine payments, not bank rails.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="id" data-m="coin"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-person"/></svg></span><b>Know your agent</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="spec" data-tag="spec" data-src="N#25 N#23" data-note="Skyfire KYAPay is an IETF draft. <li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="ctl" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-wall"/></svg></span><b>Sanctions screening</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="H#31" data-note="In production with vendor numbers. One bank sees 20,000 alerts a month.">Live</span> Every party, before release.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="ctl" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-check"/></svg></span><b>Payee name check</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="N#35 N#36" data-note="EU Verification of Payee since 9 Oct 2025, all credit transfers. UK Confirmation of Payee: 320+ organizations, Jun 2025.">Live</span> EU and UK schemes.</p></li>
<li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile" data-ly="ctl" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-ledger"/></svg></span><b>Evidence ledger</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="I#27" data-note="A Fed governor says LLMs are used for reconciliation, 29 Sep 2026. No result numbers.">Live</span> Matches payments, explains breaks.</p></li>
</ul>
</details>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<section class="pdd-scene pdd-s2-scene" id="pdd-path" data-rail="bank">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>4</b>The money path</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q">What does a payout system need, and who loses if it breaks?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">Ten states and thirteen parts carry every payout. <span class="pdd-view">Only the rail changes.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: who holds the money, and who loses.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: the states, the faults and the resend rule.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: where the stablecoin rail differs from the bank.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<div class="pdd-s2-bar"><div class="pdd-seg pdd-s2-view" data-key="view" role="group" aria-label="View"><button type="button" data-val="states" aria-pressed="true">States</button><button type="button" data-val="comp" aria-pressed="false">Components</button></div><div class="pdd-seg pdd-s2-rail" data-key="rail" role="group" aria-label="Rail for this payout"><button type="button" data-val="bank" data-m="bank" aria-pressed="true">Bank rail</button><button type="button" data-val="coin" data-m="coin" aria-pressed="false">Stablecoin rail</button></div><p class="pdd-s2-off"><span class="pdd-dot" data-m="coin"></span>The stablecoin rail is off by default. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21 E#13" data-note="No firm stablecoin payout price exists. The one independent test sent 200 USDC and found no systematic advantage. No like-for-like match with B2B payouts.">No firm stablecoin payout price exists. Ask for an all-in corridor quote.</span></p><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-run"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 2.5v11l9-5.5z"/></svg>Run a payout</button></div>
<div class="pdd-grid">
<div class="pdd-c8 pdd-s2-main">
<div class="pdd-s2-faults" data-m="risk" role="group" aria-label="Ten faults"><span class="pdd-label"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Break it</span><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="sanctions" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Sanctions match at re-screen, state 7">Sanctions match</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="wrong" aria-pressed="true" aria-label="Sent to wrong payee, state 8">Wrong payee</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="ach" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ACH returned, state 8">ACH returned</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="unknown" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Unknown outcome, state 7">Unknown outcome</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="bankexit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Partner bank exits, state 7">Bank exits</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="depeg" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Token off its peg, state 6">Token off peg</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="chain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Chain halted, state 7">Chain halted</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="wallet" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Platform wallet frozen, state 7">Wallet frozen</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="frozen" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Payee address frozen, state 8">Address frozen</button><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s2-fault" data-fault="offramp" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Off-ramp fails after deposit, state 8">Off-ramp fails</button></div><p class="pdd-cap pdd-s2-lptr">Who bears loss: open the drawer under the states.</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-trackw" style="--p:7" data-end="fault"><ol class="pdd-s2-track" aria-label="Ten payout states"><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="0" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 1, Drafted"><b>1</b><span>Drafted</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="flow"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="1" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 2, Verified"><b>2</b><span>Verified</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="flow"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="2" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 3, Approved"><b>3</b><span>Approved</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="flow"></i></li><li data-end="all"><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="3" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 4, Screened"><b>4</b><span>Screened</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="flow"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="4" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 5, Funded"><b>5</b><span>Funded</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="bank"></i></li><li data-end="coin"><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="5" data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 6, Routed"><b>6</b><span>Routed</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="bank"></i></li><li data-end="all" data-hd><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="6" data-diff data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 7, Submitted"><b>7</b><span>Submitted</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="bank"></i></li><li data-end="all" data-hd><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="7" data-diff data-done aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 8, Settled"><b>8</b><span>Settled</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="bank"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="8" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 9, Delivered"><b>9</b><span>Delivered</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="ok"></i></li><li><button type="button" class="pdd-s2-step" data-step="9" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="State 10, Reconciled"><b>10</b><span>Reconciled</span></button><i class="pdd-s2-stripe" data-m="ok"></i></li></ol><span class="pdd-s2-token" aria-hidden="true"></span></div>
<p class="pdd-s2-key"><span class="pdd-cap"><span class="pdd-dot pdd-s2-rdot"></span>States 7 to 8 change with the rail</span><span class="pdd-label">Who holds the money</span></p>
<ol class="pdd-s2-holders" aria-label="Holder of the funds at each state"><li data-m="flow" data-bank="0-3" data-coin="0-3"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-erp"/></svg><b>Payer</b><span class="pdd-label">States 1 to 4</span></li><li data-m="bank" data-bank="4-6" data-coin="4-5"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg><b>For-benefit account</b><span class="pdd-label" data-r="bank">States 5 to 7</span><span class="pdd-label" data-r="coin">States 5 to 6</span></li><li data-m="bank" data-bank="7-7" class="is-on"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg><b>Receiving bank</b><span class="pdd-label">State 8</span></li><li data-m="coin" data-coin="6-6"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-hsm"/></svg><b>Custodian wallet</b><span class="pdd-label">State 7</span></li><li data-m="coin" data-coin="7-7"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-coin"/></svg><b>Off-ramp or payee wallet</b><span class="pdd-label">State 8</span></li><li data-m="ok" data-bank="8-9" data-coin="8-9"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-person"/></svg><b>Payee</b><span class="pdd-label">States 9 to 10</span></li></ol>
</div>
<aside class="pdd-drawer pdd-c4 pdd-s2-drawer" data-open="true" data-m="risk" aria-live="polite" aria-label="Detail for the selected state or fault"><p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-dk"><span class="pdd-dot"></span><span class="pdd-s2-dkt">Fault at state 8</span></p><h3 class="pdd-s2-dt">Sent to wrong payee</h3><div class="pdd-s2-sec"><p class="pdd-label">What happens</p><p class="pdd-s2-what">One provider&#39;s RTP terms say the receiving bank does not have to send the money back when asked.</p></div><div class="pdd-s2-sec"><p class="pdd-label"><span class="pdd-s2-h2">Who bears the loss</span></p><p class="pdd-s2-two">No bearer is stated on the bank rail.</p></div><div class="pdd-s2-sec pdd-s2-rs"><p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-h3">Rule that prevents it</p><p class="pdd-s2-rule">Payee and method are verified at state 2, before approval.</p></div><p class="pdd-s2-chips"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#16" data-note="The Clearing House describes its own network. Undated page.">RTP is final and irrevocable</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#33" data-note="A payment provider's RTP terms, last updated 5 Feb 2024.">Need not return funds</span></p></aside>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s2-map" role="group" aria-label="Payout architecture. Thirteen components in four bands share one instruction model and one ledger, and only the rail adapters differ between bank and stablecoin.">
<div class="pdd-s2-band" data-m="flow">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-bandh"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Workflow</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-nodes"><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C3" data-m="flow"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-check"/></svg><span><i>C3</i>Approvals and instruction</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C1" data-m="flow"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-person"/></svg><span><i>C1</i>Payee onboarding</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C2" data-m="flow"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-sheet"/></svg><span><i>C2</i>Tax and title</span></div></div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s2-band" data-m="rule" data-stag="1">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-bandh"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Control</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-nodes"><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C4" data-m="rule"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-wall"/></svg><span><i>C4</i>Compliance screening</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C5" data-m="rule"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-flow"/></svg><span><i>C5</i>Router and gates</span></div><a class="pdd-s2-node is-ai" href="#pdd-ai" data-c="C12" data-m="rule"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-agent"/></svg><span><i>C12</i>AI assist</span></a><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C13" data-m="rule"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-risk"/></svg><span><i>C13</i>Counterparty risk</span></div></div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s2-band">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-bandh"><span class="pdd-dot" data-m="bank"></span><span class="pdd-dot" data-m="coin"></span>Rails</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-nodes"><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C6" data-m="bank"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg><span><i>C6</i>Bank rail adapters</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C7" data-m="coin"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-coin"/></svg><span><i>C7</i>Stablecoin adapter</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C8" data-m="coin"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-key"/></svg><span><i>C8</i>Custody and signing</span></div></div>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s2-band" data-m="ok">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s2-bandh"><span class="pdd-dot"></span>Record</p>
<div class="pdd-s2-nodes"><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C9" data-m="ok"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-ledger"/></svg><span><i>C9</i>Double-entry ledger</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C10" data-m="ok"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-trace"/></svg><span><i>C10</i>Reconciliation and audit</span></div><div class="pdd-s2-node" data-c="C11" data-m="ok"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-archive"/></svg><span><i>C11</i>Treasury and liquidity</span></div></div>
</div>
</div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s2-lossd"><summary><span class="pdd-view">Who bears loss</span></summary><div class="pdd-s2-lossw"><p class="pdd-s2-lnote">No contract terms exist, so every cell is a proposal.</p><table class="pdd-s2-loss"><caption class="pdd-s2-vh">Proposed bearer of the loss for twelve failures. Four rows have an undecided part.</caption><thead><tr><th scope="col"><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Failure</span></th><th scope="col"><span>Payer</span></th><th scope="col"><span>Payee</span></th><th data-loss="addr-platform" aria-pressed="false">Wrong address, platform error</button></th><td></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="bear"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Bears the loss</span></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><th scope="row"><button type="button" data-loss="addr-payee" aria-pressed="false">Wrong address confirmed by payee</button></th><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="open"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Undecided</span></td></tr><tr><th scope="row"><button type="button" data-loss="freeze-payee" aria-pressed="false">Payee address frozen after delivery</button></th><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="bear"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Bears the loss</span></td><td></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><th scope="row"><button type="button" data-loss="wallet" aria-pressed="false">Platform wallet frozen</button></th><td></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="bear"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Bears the loss</span></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><th scope="row"><button type="button" data-loss="depeg" aria-pressed="false">Depeg in transit</button></th><td></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="bear"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Bears the loss</span></td><td></td><td></td></tr><tr><th scope="row"><button type="button" data-loss="offramp" aria-pressed="false">Off-ramp fails after deposit</button></th><td></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="bear"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Bears the loss</span></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="open"></i><span class="pdd-s2-vh">Undecided</span></td></tr></tbody></table><table class="pdd-s2-loss pdd-s2-loss2"><caption scope="row"><button type="button" data-loss="fx" aria-pressed="false">FX slips against a firm quote</button></th><td></td><td></td><td></td><td><i class="pdd-s2-mk" data-k="bear"></i><span class="pdd-s2-out pdd-s2-lossout" aria-live="polite">Choose a failure to see the proposal.</p></div></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s2-legend"><summary>States and steps</summary><div class="pdd-pg-mw"><p class="pdd-pg-tl">The ten payout states are the backbone. The twelve build steps on screen 3 and its seven flow checks map onto them like this.</p><table class="pdd-pg-t"><thead><tr><th scope="col">State</th><th scope="col">Build step, screen 3</th><th scope="col">Flow check, screen 3</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><th scope="row">1 Drafted</th><td>Step 2 agent and gateway, step 3 signed request</td><td>Check 1 Intent</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">2 Verified</th><td>Step 5 payee guard, account check</td><td>Check 3 Payee guard</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">3 Approved</th><td>Step 4 mandate check, step 7 release, step 8 person releases</td><td>Check 2 Mandate check, check 5 Release matrix</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">4 Screened</th><td>Step 5 payee guard, sanctions screen, and step 6 risk score</td><td>Check 4 AI risk score</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">5 Funded</th><td>Step 9 bank rail, funds held in the for-benefit account</td><td>None</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">6 Routed</th><td>Step 6 rail picker</td><td>None</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">7 Submitted</th><td>Step 9 bank rail, step 9b stablecoin rail, step 10 card lane</td><td>Check 6 Send</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">8 Settled</th><td>Step 9, 9b or 10, whichever rail carried it</td><td>None</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">9 Delivered</th><td>Step 11 evidence ledger receives status</td><td>None</td></tr><tr><th scope="row">10 Reconciled</th><td>Step 11 evidence ledger</td><td>Check 7 Evidence</td></tr></tbody></table><p class="pdd-pg-fine">Build step 1, key registration, happens before any payout. Step 12, the kill switch, can stop a payout at any state. The eight chooser gates on screen 5 are inputs to state 6, not states.</p></div></details>
<p class="pdd-s2-sr">Bank rail shown.</p>
</div>
<p class="pdd-say">An unknown outcome is resent only after the rail or partner confirms the payment is absent, with the same key. A time limit only escalates to a person.</p>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-vg-path"><summary>Card custody</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-vault"/></svg></span><b>Card vault</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="V#1" data-note="Card vaults keep the stack out of PCI scope. VGS claim.">Live</span> Keeps the real card number. Your agent stack stays out of PCI scope.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg></span><b>Network token</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="V#6 V#1" data-note="Network tokens are in production at Visa and Mastercard.">Live</span> Stands in for the card number at each seller.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-agent"/></svg></span><b>Agentic token</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="V#6 N#18" data-note="Mastercard Agent Pay agentic tokens, consumer so far.">Pilot</span> Adds the agent, a spend cap, the allowed seller and an expiry.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">The agent holds the agentic token, never the card number.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Ask who pays when a replaced card breaks an agent payment.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Keep supplier card and bank details current. Cards get replaced often.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: The vault holder controls the card, so the agent vendor never does.</span></p></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-nw-std"><summary>Standards path</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-gateway"/></svg></span><b>Bilateral</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-src="DC#6" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026. Not a measured path.">Illustrative</span> Step 1. One bank and one platform agree their own agent rules.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="flow"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg></span><b>Consortium</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-src="DC#6" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026. Not a measured path.">Illustrative</span> Step 2. Industry groups write shared specs for agent payments.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-policy-doc"/></svg></span><b>Regulation</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="DC#6" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026. No agent payment law found.">Unsettled</span> Step 3. Rules follow once practice settles. No agent payment law yet.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-sdk"/></svg></span><b>Catalog</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-src="DC#7 DC#12" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026. Not a measured path.">Illustrative</span> Retail catalogs for agents exist. Supplier price lists for agents do not yet.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-payer"/></svg></span><b>Harmonize</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="DC#6" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026. No agent payment law found.">Unsettled</span> Each country may write its own rules. Cross border payouts feel that first.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">Private deals come first, shared specs next, law last.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Pick partners who sit in the industry groups, so you follow the spec.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Build to open specs, so a bilateral deal can move to the shared standard.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: The networks are writing the rulebook before regulators do.</span></p></details>

<section class="pdd-scene pdd-s3-scene" id="pdd-chooser" aria-label="Rail chooser">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>5</b>The rail</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q">Which rail fits this payout?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">Eight gates run on every payout, and a bank rail is the answer in most cases shown.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: the cheapest safe rail for one payout.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: the eight gates and the rail logic.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: when stablecoin wins, and what it costs.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<details class="pdd-more pdd-vg-oneoff"><summary>Card tokens</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-risk"/></svg></span><b>One-time card</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live" data-src="V#1 H#21" data-note="Single-use virtual cards are a shipped product at several issuers. VGS describes the trade-off.">Live</span> Your name shows on the statement. You handle every dispute. Repeat charges fail.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-payer"/></svg></span><b>Customer token</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="V#6 V#1" data-note="Agentic tokens tied to the customer card are in pilot.">Pilot</span> The customer&#39;s own card name shows. Disputes follow the card. Repeat charges work.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">Our read: a one-time card is a workaround. It hides the customer from the supplier.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Ask whose name your suppliers will see on each charge.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Store customer tokens so repeat charges work without a new card.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: Customer tokens keep the payer visible. One-time cards hand control to the issuer.</span></p></details>

The local payout still lands when the local system is open.","not":[{"t":"Bank partner: waits for business hours"},{"t":"Local rails: no local instant system"}],"cond":["Payee consent given","Under the platform cap, once set","Firm quote still has to beat the bank route"]},{"n":12,"rail":"Bank cross-border partner, next business day","short":"Bank cross-border partner","kind":"partner","m":"bank","role":"Bank rail","reason":"Baseline cost of 1.6%, ${cost} on ${amt}","pct":1.6,"src":["D#18"],"note":"FSB 2025 report for SME costs, data as of Mar 2025. FSB note to the G20, 12 Oct 2026: stablecoins about $300 billion, 99% dollar-pegged."},{"n":13,"rail":"Decline. Confirm payee out of band","kind":"hold","m":"risk","role":"Held","reason":"RTP terms: a receiving bank need not return funds","src":["G#33"],"note":"Terms of one payment provider for RTP. Treating wires, local instant rails and stablecoin as final in the same way is our read. A final rail also needs irreversible funds held, or a platform credit limit.","not":[{"t":"Any final rail now: payee new or changed"},{"t":"A small test payment is the other option"}]},{"n":18,"rail":"Collect bank details first","kind":"ask","m":"flow","role":"Payee onboarding","reason":"One central bank test found no systematic cost advantage","src":["D#21","E#13"],"note":"Banca d'Italia sent 200 USDC across ten corridors in 2026. Both sources report the same study.","not":[{"t":"Stablecoin: closed for this payout"}]},{"n":19,"rail":"Hold for Travel Rule data","kind":"hold","m":"risk","role":"Held","reason":"The rule applies at $3,000 or more to wires and to stablecoin transfers through a money transmitter","src":["C#27","G#20"],"note":"31 CFR 1010.100 excludes ACH, ATM and point-of-sale transfers from the Travel Rule.","not":[{"t":"ACH: outside the rule, payee data still kept","src":["G#20"]}]},{"n":20,"rail":"Hold until the bank partner recovers","kind":"hold","m":"risk","role":"Held","reason":"A 2024 Fed order made one partner bank seek approval for new fintech work","src":["G#32"],"note":"Evolve Bank, 14 Jun 2024, as reported by a trade outlet. Shown as an example of partner risk.","not":[{"t":"Any bank rail now: partner degraded"},{"t":"Stablecoin: never a fallback by default"}]},{"n":21,"rail":"Push to card, a person keys it","short":"Push to card","kind":"card","m":"bank","role":"Card rail","reason":"No agent push-to-card payout product is announced, as of 1 Nov 2026","src":["W#1","W#2","N#8","W#3"],"note":"Absence finding from a sweep of Visa Direct and Mastercard Move. Nearest items: the Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot of 20 Oct 2026, where the buyer approves each payment first, and Mastercard Agent Pay for Machines of 10 Jun 2026, which pays between agents.","not":[{"t":"Agent push to card: no product announced","src":["W#1","W#2"]},{"t":"ACH: once the payee gives bank details"}],"cond":["Unsettled: neither Visa Direct nor Mastercard Move has an agent payout product","A person keys or approves the push"]}],"gates":[{"id":"G1","name":"Sanctions screening","src":["G#16","G#17","F#8"]},{"id":"G2","name":"Token legality","src":["G#38"],"note":"Resolution BCB 561, effective 1 Oct 2026, per a law firm alert. It covers payments between a regulated eFX provider and its counterparty abroad. The central bank text was not opened. Legality was checked for Brazil alone."},{"id":"G3","name":"Consent and capability","src":["F#46","G#39"],"note":"Consent is a platform policy choice. Sources cover wallet risks."},{"id":"G4","name":"Amount caps","src":["F#16","F#17","F#20","D#4","G#4"],"note":"RTP and FedNow set a $10 million network ceiling and each bank sets a lower customer limit. Same Day ACH allows $1 million, rising to $10 million on 17 Sep 2027. The platform cap on stablecoin payouts is a placeholder with no number yet."},{"id":"G5","name":"Travel Rule data","src":["C#27","G#20"],"note":"Applies at $3,000 or more to wires and to stablecoin transfers through a money transmitter. 31 CFR 1010.100 excludes ACH, ATM and point-of-sale transfers. The gate fires for wire and stablecoin only."},{"id":"G6","name":"First payout","src":["G#33","F#16"],"note":"A first payout to a new or changed payee waits for out-of-band confirmation or a test payment before any final rail. A final rail also needs irreversible funds held, or a platform credit limit. Finality is sourced for RTP."},{"id":"G7","name":"Token allow-list","src":["C#10","F#28"],"note":"The allow-list is a platform policy choice."},{"id":"G8","name":"Partner and chain health","src":["G#28","G#32"],"note":"A degraded chain or ramp closes the stablecoin route. A degraded bank partner closes bank rails."}],"presets":{"us-next":{"route":"domestic","country":"us","status":"established","cap":"instant","consent":"no","amount":1,"speed":"next","day":"hours","health":"normal","token":"usdc","data":"yes"},"new-us":{"route":"domestic","country":"us","status":"new","cap":"instant","consent":"no","amount":1,"speed":"minutes","day":"hours","health":"normal","token":"usdc","data":"yes"},"contractor":{"route":"cross","country":"other","status":"established","cap":"hosted","consent":"yes","amount":0,"speed":"same","day":"hours","health":"normal","token":"usdc","data":"yes"},"brazil":{"route":"cross","country":"br","status":"established","cap":"ach","consent":"yes","amount":1,"speed":"minutes","day":"outside","health":"normal","token":"usdc","data":"yes"}},"readers":{"buyer":"us-next","builder":"new-us","strategy":"contractor"},"bands":["Under $3,000","$3,000 to $1 million","Above $1 million to $10 million","Above $10 million"],"closeSrc":{"coin":{"src":["D#21"],"note":"The one independent test used 200 USDC transfers. <p class="pdd-s3-dec"><b class="pdd-s3-dg" data-m="flow">Deciding check</b> <span class="pdd-s3-why pdd-view">Next day is enough, so the cheapest bank rail</span></p>

<p class="pdd-s3-pcap" aria-live="polite"><b class="pdd-s3-pk">2 of 5.</b> <span class="pdd-s3-pt">The same supplier at $20,000. ACH still costs $0.26 to $0.50, since its fee does not grow with size. Percentage prices belong to cross-border rails, so they step back.</span></p>

<p class="pdd-s3-lsr" aria-live="polite"></p>
</div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s3-dw" id="pdd-s3-dw"><summary>Inputs and gates</summary><div class="pdd-s3-dwb">
<div class="pdd-s3-form">
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Route</span><select data-key="route"><option value="domestic" selected>Domestic US</option><option value="cross">Cross-border</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Payee country</span><select data-key="country"><option value="us" selected>United States</option><option value="br">Brazil</option><option value="other">Other</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Payee status</span><select data-key="status"><option value="established" selected>Established</option><option value="new">New</option><option value="changed">Changed</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Payee can receive</span><select data-key="cap"><option value="instant" selected>Instant bank</option><option value="ach">ACH-only bank</option><option value="hosted">Hosted wallet</option><option value="self">Self-hosted wallet</option><option value="card">Card only</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Stablecoin consent</span><select data-key="consent"><option value="yes">Yes</option><option value="no" selected>No</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Token asked for</span><select data-key="token"><option value="usdc" selected>USDC</option><option value="usdt">USDT</option></select></label>
<div class="pdd-s3-f pdd-s3-fw" data-m="flow"><label for="pdd-s3-amt">Amount</label><input type="range" id="pdd-s3-amt" min="0" max="3" step="1" value="1" aria-valuetext="$3,000 to $1 million"><output class="pdd-cap pdd-s3-amt-out" for="pdd-s3-amt">$3,000 to $1 million</output></div>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Travel Rule data</span><select data-key="data"><option value="yes" selected>Complete</option><option value="no">Incomplete</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Speed</span><select data-key="speed"><option value="minutes">Minutes</option><option value="same">Same day</option><option value="next" selected>Next day</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Day</span><select data-key="day"><option value="hours" selected>Business hours</option><option value="outside">Weekend or holiday</option></select></label>
<label class="pdd-s3-f"><span>Partner and chain</span><select data-key="health"><option value="normal" selected>Normal</option><option value="chain">Chain or ramp down</option><option value="bank">Bank partner down</option></select></label>
<p class="pdd-s3-shut pdd-s3-fw">Shut at a weekend or holiday: <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#8 G#6" data-note="Fedwire runs Monday to Friday. Sundays and weekday holidays come no earlier than 2028.">Fedwire</span>,</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#25 D#27" data-note="Circle says redemption payouts to a bank typically settle on the next business day. USDC traded as low as 86 cents over a weekend in Mar 2023 while redemption was paused.">USDC redemption to a bank</span></p>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s3-stack">
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s3-gl">Eight gates, all run</p>
<div class="pdd-s3-gates" role="group" aria-label="Gates, in order">
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G1" data-s="info" data-m="rule" aria-pressed="true" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G1</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Sanctions screening</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Runs</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G2" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G2</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Token legality</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G3" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G3</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Consent and capability</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G4" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G4</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Amount caps</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G5" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G5</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Travel Rule data</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G6" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G6</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">First payout</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G7" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G7</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Token allow-list</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
<button type="button" class="pdd-s3-g" data-g="G8" data-s="pass" data-m="ok" aria-pressed="false" aria-controls="pdd-s3-note"><b>G8</b><span class="pdd-s3-gn">Partner and chain health</span><span class="pdd-s3-gs">Pass</span></button>
</div>
<p class="pdd-s3-note" id="pdd-s3-note" data-m="rule"><b class="pdd-s3-nn">G1 Sanctions screening</b> <span class="pdd-chip pdd-s3-gw" data-src="G#16 G#17 F#8">Parties and addresses, again at submission</span></p>
<p class="pdd-label pdd-s3-gl">Why not the others</p>
<ul class="pdd-s3-not"><li>Same Day ACH: speed not needed</li><li><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#16" data-note="The Clearing House network page, undated.">RTP or FedNow: irrevocable, no need</span></li><li>Stablecoin: never chosen for domestic</li></ul>
</div>
</div></details>
<p class="pdd-s3-sr" aria-live="polite"></p>
</div>
<p class="pdd-say"><span class="pdd-view">Our read: mapping inputs to a rail is ours, not a vendor rule.</span> Stripe lists <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#27" data-note="Stripe stablecoin payouts are in private preview with no published price. Its 1.5% list price is for accepting stablecoin payments, which is a different service.">1.5%</span> to accept stablecoin payments and no price for payouts.</p>
</section>
<section class="pdd-scene pdd-s5-scene" id="pdd-rules" aria-label="Rules and open questions">
<p class="pdd-kicker"><b>6</b>Rules and open questions</p>
<h2 class="pdd-q">What do the rules say, and what stays open?</h2>
<p class="pdd-a">The OCC issued its final GENIUS Act issuer rule, under the 2025 US stablecoin law, on <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2" data-note="OCC final rule for stablecoin issuers, issued 21 Oct 2026, effective 18 Jan 2027. AML and sanctions parts come in a separate Treasury rulemaking.">21 Oct 2026</span>. The other four agencies are still at proposal, and no rule blocks paying a supplier in an <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#10" data-note="The rules fall mainly on issuers.">existing stablecoin</span>.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: which rules change your payouts, and when.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: the rules to build in before launch.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">For you: the dates that move the market.</span></p>
<div class="pdd-stage">
<details class="pdd-more pdd-wr-rel" data-open-for="all"><summary>Who releases money</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-payer"/></svg></span><b>Person releases</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="live" data-tag="live">Live</span> The default in every shipped product. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#4" data-note="Stripe Treasury for agents, 21 Oct 2026. The MCP tools send_money, transfer_money and convert_currency each need human confirmation in the Dashboard, with an approval token that expires in 24 hours.">Stripe Treasury</span> asks a person to confirm each send. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#7 N#32" data-note="PYMNTS, 21 Aug 2026: the Coupa agent does everything up to the last step but does not have the final say.">Coupa</span>, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#8" data-note="Ramp support article: automatic bill creation does not approve a bill or bypass any approvers.">Ramp</span> and <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#9" data-note="Oracle Fusion Cloud ERP 26B Payables Agent routes for approval and payment. Read through trade press, the Oracle blog returned 403.">Oracle</span> stop at the approval queue. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#5" data-note="Airwallex AgentOS docs: components do not initiate money-out actions such as transfers, FX conversions or payouts.">Airwallex</span> agents never send money out.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-agent"/></svg></span><b>Agent releases</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot">Pilot</span> Four cases found, each inside limits a person set. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#10" data-note="Meow blog, 10 Jun 2026. Keys default to read and request-to-spend. An agent spends on its own only if the account holder turns it on, inside per-agent limits. Vendor page.">Meow</span> spend keys the account holder switches on, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#11" data-note="Payouts.com Digital Employee, 14 Jul 2026: hard spend limits, multi-party sign-off on large wires, two weeks of shadow mode first. Trade press.">Payouts.com</span> after two weeks in shadow mode, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="W#12" data-note="Tipalti AP Agent, 22 Oct 2026. Finance teams set which activities the agent handles independently and which need human approval.">Tipalti</span> configurable autonomy, and the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#8" data-note="Visa, BILL and Billtrust pilot, 20 Oct 2026. The agent only executes payments the buyer has already approved.">Visa, BILL and Billtrust</span> pilot after buyer approval.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-bank"/></svg></span><b>Banks</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled">Unsettled</span> No large US bank offers a client agent that releases a payment. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#12 W#13" data-note="J.P. Morgan Payments: Mirakl agentic commerce pilots on the merchant side, 10 Mar 2026, and treasury commentary that high-value payment authorization is the clearest test case. No payables agent.">JPMorgan</span> runs merchant pilots and writes about the test case. <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#19" data-note="HSBC Singapore, first live B2B agentic payment on 29 May 2026, announced 9 Jun 2026, a proof of concept on Mastercard Agent Pay with controls and oversight.">HSBC Singapore</span> ran the one bank pilot between a buyer and a supplier, May 2026, still a proof of concept.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">Agents prepare. People release. Four vendors let the agent go last.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Ask each vendor to show the screen where a person releases, and who can switch it off.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Put the release step behind its own key and limit, never inside the agent prompt.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: The release split matters more than rail coverage, and it will decide who wins payouts.</span></p></details>
<div class="pdd-grid">
<ul class="pdd-s5-ags" aria-label="Five US agencies, GENIUS rule status">
<li class="pdd-s5-ag pdd-panel" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s5-agtop"><svg class="pdd-s5-glyph pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-policy"/></svg><b class="pdd-s5-name">OCC</b></div><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Proposed</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#11">25 Feb 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s5-line" data-m="ok"><span>Final rule</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2" data-note="Issued 21 Oct 2026, effective 18 Jan 2027. AML and sanctions parts come in a separate Treasury rulemaking.">21 Oct 2026</span></p></li>
<li class="pdd-s5-ag pdd-panel" data-m="rule" data-rise><div class="pdd-s5-agtop"><svg class="pdd-s5-glyph pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#pdd-ar3-i-policy"/></svg><b class="pdd-s5-name">Federal Reserve</b></div><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Proposed</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#13" data-note="Two proposals.">24 Sep 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s5-line"><span>Comments close</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#14">30 Nov 2026</span></p></li>

Comments close 19 Oct 2026.">18 Aug 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s5-line" data-m="ok"><span>Interim final rule, procedural only</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#2 G#2" data-note="Effective, with certifications not yet accepted.">30 Sep 2026</span></p></li>

</ul>
