<div class="pdd-s5-qs pdd-c4">
<p class="pdd-label">Three open questions for your seat</p>
<ol class="pdd-s5-ql" data-for="buyer"><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">1</b><p class="pdd-cap">What is the firm <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21" data-note="One central bank test found no systematic advantage.">all-in quote</span> for this corridor?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-chooser">Screen 5: which rail fits</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">2</b><p class="pdd-cap">Who bears <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#31" data-note="One failure left users short. The cause was not known.">each loss</span>?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-path">Screen 4: the money path</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">3</b><p class="pdd-cap">Which AI result has <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#13" data-note="One vendor figure rests on a single bill.">evidence</span> beyond the vendor?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2: where AI fits</a></li></ol>
<ol class="pdd-s5-ql" data-for="builder"><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">1</b><p class="pdd-cap">Can one instruction ever <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#29" data-note="Stripe says keys can be pruned after 24 hours.">send twice</span>?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-path">Screen 4: the money path</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">2</b><p class="pdd-cap">Which gate does an agent never <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#30" data-note="No live bank deployment found releases a payment unsupervised.">pass alone</span>?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2: where AI fits</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">3</b><p class="pdd-cap">Which rails carry the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#20 C#27" data-note="Wires and money transmitter transfers at $3,000 or more. 31 CFR 1010.100 excludes ACH, ATM and point-of-sale transfers.">Travel Rule</span> at $3,000?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-chooser">Screen 5: which rail fits</a></li></ol>
<ol class="pdd-s5-ql" data-for="strategy"><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">1</b><p class="pdd-cap">Who prices AI <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#21" data-note="BILL lists no separate price for its agents.">per outcome</span> first?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2: where AI fits</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">2</b><p class="pdd-cap">Do falling <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="J#5">take rates</span> outrun volume growth?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-answer">Screen 1: the answer</a></li><li class="pdd-s5-qc"><b class="pdd-s5-qn" aria-hidden="true">3</b><p class="pdd-cap">Does the FCA change rules for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="I#31" data-note="Wording from a law firm summary.">agent payments</span>?</p><a class="pdd-pill" href="#pdd-ai">Screen 2: where AI fits</a></li></ol>
</div>
<div class="pdd-s5-foot">
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s5-deals" id="pdd-s5-deals"><summary>Who buys what</summary><ul class="pdd-s5-dl" aria-label="Eight payout deals, newest first"><li data-m="flow"><span class="pdd-s5-db"><b>Anchorage Digital</b> bought Routable</span><span class="pdd-s5-dd">6 Oct 2026</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#4 A#2" data-note="Buyer release, 6 Oct 2026. Terms not disclosed.">Workflow</span></li><li data-m="flow"><span class="pdd-s5-db"><b>Valley National</b> agreed to buy Bluevine</span><span class="pdd-s5-dd">Sep 2026</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="A#25" data-note="About $340 million, pending.">Workflow</span></li><li data-m="rule"><span class="pdd-s5-db"><b>Chime</b> agreed to buy Stride Bank</span><span class="pdd-s5-dd">Sep 2026</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="A#24" data-note="$590 million cash, pending.">Charter</span></li><li data-m="coin"><span class="pdd-s5-db"><b>Mastercard</b> bought BVNK</span><span class="pdd-s5-dd">3 Aug 2026</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="B#13 B#14" data-note="Closed 3 Aug 2026. Up to $1.8 billion, $300 million contingent.">Rail</span></li><li data-m="flow"><span class="pdd-s5-db"><b>Xero</b> bought Melio</span><span class="pdd-s5-dd">Oct 2025</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#10" data-note="$2.5 billion. No stablecoin mention.">Workflow</span></li><li data-m="flow"><span class="pdd-s5-db"><b>TPG and Corpay</b> bought AvidXchange</span><span class="pdd-s5-dd">Oct 2025</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#9" data-note="About $2.2 billion. No stablecoin mention.">Workflow</span></li><li data-m="risk"><span class="pdd-s5-db"><b>Coinbase</b> and BVNK, talks ended</span><span class="pdd-s5-dd">Nov 2025</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#11" data-note="About $2 billion. Talks ended.">Failed</span></li><li data-m="coin"><span class="pdd-s5-db"><b>Stripe</b> bought Bridge</span><span class="pdd-s5-dd">Feb 2025</span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="A#21" data-note="$1.1 billion, closed.">Rail</span></li></ul><p class="pdd-s5-dn"><span class="pdd-view">The largest workflow deals carry no stablecoin reason in their own sources.</span></p></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s5-cant"><summary>Honest limits</summary><ol class="pdd-s5-not"><li>A like-for-like cost for a stablecoin payout at any amount.</li><li>Any vendor AI result checked independently.</li><li>One market size. Analyst figures conflict.</li><li>Contract terms behind who bears each loss.</li><li>A measured saving from AI rail or FX choice.</li><li>Payee-country legality beyond Brazil.</li></ol></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s5-ask" id="pdd-s5-ask"><summary>Questions to ask</summary><p class="pdd-label">Your bank</p><ol class="pdd-bd-ql"><li>Will you let our agent start a payment, or <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10">read only</span>?</li><li>How do agent limits enter our <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#6">security procedure agreement</span>?</li><li>Do you issue <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#19 N#18">agent tokens</span> on Visa or Mastercard?</li><li>Who carries the loss when an agent <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#10 N#11" data-note="BofA is working out who is liable when an AI authorizes a payment. JPMorgan's CFO said customers who share credentials with agents could be responsible.">pays the wrong supplier</span>?</li><li>How does your <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5">Nacha fraud monitoring</span> treat our agent's payments?</li><li>As an Originator, how do we meet our own <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#5">monitoring duty</span>?</li><li>Does our insurance cover agent-initiated transfers? Ask the insurer too.</li></ol><p class="pdd-label">Your vendors</p><ol class="pdd-bd-ql"><li>Which of the three checks on an agent payment do you cover today: card is real, owner or agent, agent got it right?</li><li>Which tokens do you accept now, not which protocols you plan?</li><li>Whose name will our suppliers see on each charge?</li><li>Who pays when a replaced card breaks an agent payment?</li><li>Who pays when your agent pays the wrong supplier?</li><li>Show us the screen where a person releases, and who can switch the agent off.</li><li>How fast does your kill switch freeze a supplier payout?</li><li>What is the firm <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21" data-note="One central bank test found no systematic advantage for stablecoins.">all-in quote</span> for this corridor, on every rail you offer?</li><li>Which AI result has <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="H#13" data-note="One vendor figure rests on a single bill.">evidence</span> beyond your own numbers?</li><li>What is your dated quantum safe plan, and will you put it in the contract?</li><li>Which industry groups do you sit in, so we follow the shared spec?</li></ol></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-s1-note">
<summary>What changed</summary><p class="pdd-cap pdd-s5-counts" data-for="strategy">The first research note, rechecked, 20 statements: <span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="yes">Supported 5</span> <span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="part">Partly supported 9</span> <span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="stale">Stale 1</span> <span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="opinion">Unsourced opinion 2</span> <span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="open">Unsettled 3</span></p>
<div class="pdd-s1-audit">
<div class="pdd-s1-keys" role="group" aria-label="Show statements by verdict"><button type="button" class="pdd-s1-key" data-s1-key="yes" aria-pressed="false"><span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="yes">Supported</span><b class="pdd-s1-count">5</b></button><button type="button" class="pdd-s1-key" data-s1-key="part" aria-pressed="false"><span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="part">Partly supported</span><b class="pdd-s1-count">9</b></button><button type="button" class="pdd-s1-key" data-s1-key="stale" aria-pressed="false"><span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="stale">Stale</span><b class="pdd-s1-count">1</b></button><button type="button" class="pdd-s1-key" data-s1-key="opinion" aria-pressed="false"><span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="opinion">Unsourced opinion</span><b class="pdd-s1-count">2</b></button><button type="button" class="pdd-s1-key" data-s1-key="open" aria-pressed="false"><span class="pdd-stamp" data-k="open">Unsettled</span><b class="pdd-s1-count">3</b></button></div>
<ol class="pdd-s1-lines"><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Workflow bought, stablecoins as rail</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span>The <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="G#9" data-note="TPG and Corpay completed the acquisition, about $2.2 billion.">AvidXchange release</span> does not mention stablecoins</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="yes"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Anchorage acquired Routable, <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#4" data-note="Anchorage Digital release, 6 Oct 2026.">6 Oct 2026</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Supported</em><span>No price published</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="yes"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Federally chartered crypto bank</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Supported</em><span>An OCC <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="A#8" data-note="Conditional approval dated 13 Jan 2021.">national trust bank</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="opinion"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Workflow control beats the rail</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Unsourced opinion</em><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#6" data-note="The release frames the logic as issuance plus distribution.">No source found</span> measures it</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Payouts slow and fee-heavy</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span>Holds for <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#18 D#2" data-note="FSB 2025 report for SME costs, data as of Mar 2025. AFP paper check medians cited by Nacha.">SME cross-border and paper checks</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="open"><p class="pdd-s1-st">CFOs and AP teams feel it</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Unsettled</em><span>Surveys measure <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#31" data-note="AFP survey of 465 treasury practitioners, calendar 2025.">fraud</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="yes"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Visa extended stablecoin settlement</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Supported</em><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="B#2" data-note="Card settlement between issuers, acquirers and Visa. It is not supplier payout.">Issuers and acquirers</span> settling with Visa</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Visa run rate <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="B#1" data-note="Card settlement between issuers, acquirers and Visa. It is not supplier payout.">$7 billion</span></p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span>Now <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="O#30" data-note="Visa, 8 Sep 2026: stablecoin settlement passed a $20 billion annualized run rate. Card settlement, not supplier payout.">over $20 billion</span> annualized, card settlement</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="open"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Source dated 10 Jun, no year</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Unsettled</em><span>The release is dated <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="B#1" data-note="Visa release, 29 Apr 2026, on stablecoin settlement.">29 Apr 2026</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="stale"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Mastercard pilot-stage</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Stale</em><span>Closed BVNK on <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="B#13" data-note="The closing release gives no price.">3 Aug 2026</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Startup against incumbent</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span>Buyer valued at <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="A#18" data-note="Crypto Briefing reports this, after a Feb 2026 investment.">$4.2 billion</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="yes"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Chartered bank buying workflow</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Supported</em><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="A#1" data-note="Anchorage Digital, a federally chartered crypto bank, buying Routable.">One case</span> in a sample</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Networks add settlement as cost-down</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span>No <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="F#27" data-note="Stripe&#39;s stablecoin payouts are in private preview.">published price</span> found shows a saving</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">AI role peripheral</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span>Peripheral to <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#39" data-note="Deal counsel release and trade press. AI is not named as a reason.">this deal</span>.</span> AI leads in <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="E#6" data-note="BILL&#39;s own framing of its product direction.">AP products</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="opinion"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Stablecoin rail is the real change</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Unsourced opinion</em><span>One test found no <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="D#21" data-note="Banca d&#39;Italia, 200 USDC across ten corridors, 2026.">systematic advantage</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">GENIUS rules not final</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2 C#13 F#1 C#16 C#17" data-note="The OCC issued its final issuer rule on 21 Oct 2026. The Federal Reserve, Treasury, FDIC and NCUA rules are still proposals.">OCC final</span>, four agencies pending</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Licensing uncertain</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="N#2" data-note="OCC final rule for stablecoin issuers, issued 21 Oct 2026, effective 18 Jan 2027. AML and sanctions parts come in a separate Treasury rulemaking.">OCC issuer rule</span> final, 21 Oct 2026</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="part"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Reserve treatment uncertain</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Partly supported</em><span><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="C#4 C#5" data-note="GENIUS Act, enacted 18 Jul 2025: one-to-one reserves and no yield to holders.">Backing, yield ban</span> enacted</span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="yes"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Early commercial, sector</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Supported</em><span>Surveys: <span class="pdd-nb"><span class="pdd-chip" data-src="E#11" data-note="AFP, Mar 2026. Includes tokenized products.">1% of treasurers</span>,</span> <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="E#12" data-note="EY-Parthenon, 350 executives, Jun 2025.">9% of corporates</span></span></p></li><li class="pdd-s1-line" data-k="open"><p class="pdd-s1-st">Early commercial, this deal</p><p class="pdd-s1-vd"><em class="pdd-s1-word">Unsettled</em><span>Native settlement &ldquo;over the <span class="pdd-chip" data-src="A#5" data-note="Banking Dive. No dated launch is given.">coming quarters</span>&rdquo;</span></p></li></ol>
</div>
</details>
<div class="pdd-s5-copy"><button type="button" class="pdd-pill pdd-s5-copybtn">Copy link</button><span class="pdd-s5-copied" role="status" aria-live="polite"></span></div>
</div>
</div>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-vg-err"><summary>Agent errors</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-appeal"/></svg></span><b>Dispute code</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="V#10" data-note="No network-wide dispute reason code for agent errors was found.">Unsettled</span> No network has a reason code for agent errors. Sellers carry the risk by default.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="ok"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-approve"/></svg></span><b>Amex cover</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="V#2 O#11" data-note="Amex Agent Purchase Protection, Apr 2026, for registered agents inside the ACE developer kit, which is still a kit for select developers.">Pilot</span> Covers errors by registered agents since Apr 2026. The only network so far.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-explain"/></svg></span><b>Evidence gap</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="V#1" data-note="Dispute evidence today is IP address and device, which belong to the agent server. VGS claim.">Unsettled</span> The IP address and device belong to the agent&#39;s server, not the customer.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="64" height="64" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-gateway"/></svg></span><b>Platform gates</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="V#4" data-note="Ninth Circuit vacated the Amazon v Perplexity injunction in Aug 2026. The case continues.">Unsettled</span> Amazon blocked a Perplexity agent. An appeals court lifted the ban in Aug 2026. The case goes on.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">Who pays for an agent mistake stays open.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Ask vendors who pays when their agent pays the wrong supplier.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Keep agent logs as dispute evidence, since IP and device prove nothing.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: Supplier platforms now decide which agents get in.</span></p></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-nw-risk"><summary>Next wave risks</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-security"/></svg></span><b>Kill switch</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="DC#6 DC#9" data-note="Spend caps and allowlists exist. No single stop control was found.">Unsettled</span> One control that freezes an agent wallet in seconds. No common standard yet.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-policy-doc"/></svg></span><b>Liability split</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="DC#6 DC#10" data-note="UK APP cover reaches consumers and small firms only, 85,000 GBP cap.">Unsettled</span> Was it a wallet fault or a vague owner limit? Few contracts say.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-risk"/></svg></span><b>Velocity risk</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="DC#8" data-note="Sarah Breeden, Bank of England, 30 Jun 2026: herding by AI agents could amplify market volatility. Agentic payments were discussed as consent, disputes and liability, not as a run risk.">Unsettled</span> Agents that react alike could amplify market swings, the Bank of England says. For payments the open questions are consent, disputes and liability.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-appeal"/></svg></span><b>Consent withdrawal</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="DC#6" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026.">Unsettled</span> An owner pulls consent mid task. Payments already queued may still go out.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">Money moves faster than the rules about who stops it.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Ask each vendor how fast their kill switch freezes a supplier payout.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Put the kill switch in wallet policy, not inside the agent prompt.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: Liability will settle in contracts long before it settles in law.</span></p></details>
<details class="pdd-more pdd-nw-pqc"><summary>Quantum readiness</summary><ul class="pdd-bd-tiles pdd-vg-tiles"><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-vault"/></svg></span><b>Crypto inventory</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="DC#14" data-note="NIST IR 8547 final, Oct 2026, sets the 2030 and 2035 dates an inventory works to.">Pilot</span> List every key, certificate and cipher your payout flow uses.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="risk"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-explain"/></svg></span><b>Harvest risk</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="open" data-tag="unsettled" data-src="DC#7" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026.">Unsettled</span> Data stolen today can be read later. Supplier bank details stay useful for years.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="rule"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-policy-doc"/></svg></span><b>Vendor PQC clause</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="illus" data-tag="illustrative" data-src="DC#7" data-note="Panel speaker view, DC Fintech Week, Oct 2026.">Illustrative</span> Ask banks and vendors for a dated quantum safe plan in the contract.</p></li><li class="pdd-panel pdd-bd-tile pdd-vg-tile" data-m="bank"><div class="pdd-bd-th"><span class="pdd-bd-sico"><svg class="pdd-iso" viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="44" height="44" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><use href="#i-network"/></svg></span><b>Rail upgrades</b></div><p><span class="pdd-chip pdd-bd-st" data-st="pilot" data-tag="pilot" data-src="DC#13 DC#14" data-note="BIS Project Leap phase 2 tested quantum safe signatures in test scenarios on an operational Eurosystem payment system. NIST IR 8547 final, 27 Oct 2026: RSA and ECC deprecated after 2030, disallowed after 2035.">Pilot</span> The BIS tested quantum safe signatures on an operational payment system. Old keys phase out from 2030.</p></li></ul><p class="pdd-say">Start with the list, then the contracts.<span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="buyer">For you: Add a quantum safe clause to the next bank or vendor renewal.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="builder">For you: Keep crypto swappable so new algorithms drop in without a rewrite.</span><span class="pdd-seatline" data-for="strategy">Our read: Quantum work is a contracts and inventory job first, not a science job.</span></p></details>
